Create `scripts/audioRender.ts`:

```ts
// CLI audio for `npm run video` (AUDIO-005): stages the bundled files next to the
// staged local ones, measures every staged file, refuses to render while any
// audio source is missing (AINV-5), and decodes every staged file once with
// ffmpeg before Remotion is spawned (AINV-8).
//
// Remotion reads only the staged copies under `__local-assets/`: a change to
// `public/audio/` or to `local-assets/audio/` after staging cannot reach the render.

import {mkdir, readFile, stat, writeFile} from "node:fs/promises";
import {dirname, extname, join} from "node:path";
import {
  bundledAudioCatalog,
  isBundledAudio,
  type AudioDurationMap,
  type AudioRef,
  type BundledAudio,
} from "../src/audio/catalog.ts";
import {MAX_AUDIO_STORY_SECONDS} from "../src/audio/constants.ts";
import {compileAudioPlan} from "../src/audio/plan.ts";
import {
  audioFileUnusableMessage,
  bundledAudioMismatchMessage,
  bundledAudioUnavailableMessage,
  describeAudioRefRoles,
  describeAudioRole,
} from "../src/audio/readiness.ts";
import {
  audioSourceState,
  buildAudioSourceMap,
  resolveAudioSrc,
  type AudioSource,
  type AudioSourceMap,
} from "../src/audio/sources.ts";
import {storyAudioDurations, storyAudioRefs} from "../src/audio/storyAudio.ts";
import {inspectAudio} from "../src/localAssets/audioInspection.ts";
import {isLocalAudioRef} from "../src/localAssets/refs.ts";
import {
  STAGED_LOCAL_ASSETS_DIR,
  type LocalAssetSourceMap,
} from "../src/localAssets/sources.ts";
import {compileTimeline} from "../src/story/timeline.ts";
import type {Story} from "../src/story/types.ts";
import {VIDEO_FPS} from "../src/videoConfig.ts";
import {checkAudioFileDecodes, type AudioPreflightDeps} from "./audioPreflight.ts";

/** A bundled file must be as long as its catalog says (D-8), within this much. */
const BUNDLED_DURATION_TOLERANCE_SECONDS = 0.001;

/** The staged bundled copies live in their own folder next to `audio/` (staged local files). */
export const STAGED_BUNDLED_AUDIO_DIR = `${STAGED_LOCAL_ASSETS_DIR}/bundled`;

/** The render cannot start because of its audio; the message says why and for which role. */
export class AudioRenderError extends Error {
  override name = "AudioRenderError";
}

export type CliAudioOptions = {
  /** The command's private public directory (a copy of `public/`; staged files are added to it). */
  publicDir: string;
  /** `stageLocalAssetsForStory(...).sources`: the static paths of the staged local files. */
  localSources: LocalAssetSourceMap;
  /** What a person calls a staged local file (its path in `local-assets/audio/`), by ref. */
  localNames?: ReadonlyMap<string, string>;
  /** Where the bundled files are read from. Defaults to the repository `public/`. */
  publicSourceDir?: string;
  /** Called between files so an interrupt stops the work early. */
  checkInterrupt?: () => void;
  /** The command's interrupt guard: the decode children are attached to it, so a signal stops them. */
  control?: AudioPreflightDeps["control"];
  preflight?: AudioPreflightDeps;
};

export type CliAudioInputs = {
  audioDurations: AudioDurationMap;
  audioSources: AudioSourceMap;
};

type StagedAudio = {
  ref: AudioRef;
  /** What the error messages call the file. */
  name: string;
  /** Absolute path of the staged copy. */
  path: string;
};

const unusable = (
  name: string,
  roles: readonly string[],
  reason: string,
): AudioRenderError =>
  new AudioRenderError(
    [
      audioFileUnusableMessage(name),
      `  used by: ${roles.join(", ")}`,
      `  reason: ${reason}`,
    ].join("\n"),
  );

const readableFile = async (path: string): Promise<Uint8Array | null> => {
  try {
    if (!(await stat(path)).isFile()) {
      return null;
    }

    return await readFile(path);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
};

/** Copies the bundled file of `id` into the staging directory; null when this build has no such file. */
const stageBundledFile = async (
  id: BundledAudio,
  options: {publicDir: string; publicSourceDir: string},
): Promise<{source: AudioSource; staged: string | null}> => {
  const {file} = bundledAudioCatalog[id];

  if (file === null) {
    return {source: {kind: "unavailable"}, staged: null};
  }

  const bytes = await readableFile(join(options.publicSourceDir, ...file.split("/")));

  if (bytes === null) {
    return {source: {kind: "unavailable"}, staged: null};
  }

  const stagedPath = `${STAGED_BUNDLED_AUDIO_DIR}/${id}${extname(file)}`;
  const absolute = join(options.publicDir, ...stagedPath.split("/"));

  await mkdir(dirname(absolute), {recursive: true});
  await writeFile(absolute, bytes);

  return {source: {kind: "static", path: stagedPath}, staged: absolute};
};

const planProblems = (story: Story, audioDurations: AudioDurationMap, audioSources: AudioSourceMap): string[] => {
  const plan = compileAudioPlan({
    story,
    timeline: compileTimeline(story, VIDEO_FPS, audioDurations),
    fps: VIDEO_FPS,
    audioDurations,
    sourceState: (ref) =>
      audioSourceState(resolveAudioSrc(ref, audioSources, "explicit")),
  });
  const problems: string[] = [];

  for (const issue of plan.issues) {
    if (issue.kind === "missing-audio") {
      problems.push(
        `  ${describeAudioRole(issue.role, issue.sceneIndex)}: ${
          isBundledAudio(issue.ref)
            ? bundledAudioUnavailableMessage(issue.ref)
            : `Audio file ${issue.ref} is missing.`
        }`,
      );
    } else if (issue.kind === "pending-audio") {
      // Staged sources are never pending: this is a bug, not a user error.
      problems.push(
        `  ${describeAudioRole(issue.role, issue.sceneIndex)}: internal error, the source of ${issue.ref} is still pending.`,
      );
    } else if (issue.kind === "story-too-long-for-audio") {
      problems.push(
        `  This Story is longer than ${MAX_AUDIO_STORY_SECONDS} s, the limit for Stories with audio.`,
      );
    }
  }

  return problems;
};

/**
 * Everything the render of a Story with audio needs before Remotion starts:
 * the props' `audioDurations` and `audioSources`. Throws `AudioRenderError`
 * (and leaves nothing for Remotion to read) when any audio source is missing
 * or fails the metadata or decode checks.
 */
export const prepareCliAudio = async (
  story: Story,
  options: CliAudioOptions,
): Promise<CliAudioInputs> => {
  const checkInterrupt = options.checkInterrupt ?? (() => undefined);
  const publicSourceDir = options.publicSourceDir ?? "public";
  const refs = storyAudioRefs(story);
  const staged: StagedAudio[] = [];
  const bundledSources: Partial<Record<BundledAudio, AudioSource>> = {};

  // 1. Bundled files are staged before anything is inspected.
  for (const ref of refs) {
    if (!isBundledAudio(ref)) {
      continue;
    }

    checkInterrupt();

    const result = await stageBundledFile(ref, {
      publicDir: options.publicDir,
      publicSourceDir,
    });

    bundledSources[ref] = result.source;

    if (result.staged !== null) {
      staged.push({ref, name: ref, path: result.staged});
    }
  }

  // The staged local copies were written by stageLocalAssetsForStory.
  for (const ref of refs) {
    const source = isLocalAudioRef(ref) ? options.localSources[ref] : undefined;

    if (source?.kind === "static") {
      staged.push({
        ref,
        name: options.localNames?.get(ref) ?? ref,
        path: join(options.publicDir, ...source.path.split("/")),
      });
    }
  }

  // 2. Metadata of every staged file (AINV-7); a local file's duration is the
  // one the timeline uses (AINV-3), a bundled file must match its catalog entry.
  const localDurations: Partial<Record<AudioRef, number>> = {};
  const durationOf = new Map<AudioRef, number>();

  for (const file of staged) {
    checkInterrupt();

    const bytes = await readableFile(file.path);
    const roles = describeAudioRefRoles(story, file.ref);

    if (bytes === null) {
      throw unusable(file.name, roles, "the staged copy could not be read");
    }

    const inspected = await inspectAudio(bytes);

    if (!inspected.ok) {
      throw unusable(file.name, roles, inspected.message);
    }

    const seconds = inspected.metadata.durationSeconds;

    if (isBundledAudio(file.ref)) {
      const expected = bundledAudioCatalog[file.ref].durationSeconds;

      if (
        expected === null ||
        Math.abs(seconds - expected) > BUNDLED_DURATION_TOLERANCE_SECONDS
      ) {
        throw new AudioRenderError(bundledAudioMismatchMessage(file.ref));
      }
    } else {
      localDurations[file.ref] = seconds;
    }

    durationOf.set(file.ref, seconds);
  }

  // 3. The plan with the very sources the render will read.
  const audioDurations = storyAudioDurations(story, localDurations);
  const audioSources = buildAudioSourceMap(
    story,
    options.localSources,
    (id) => bundledSources[id],
  );
  const problems = planProblems(story, audioDurations, audioSources);

  if (problems.length > 0) {
    throw new AudioRenderError(
      ["Cannot render Story: some of its audio is not available.", "", ...problems].join(
        "\n",
      ),
    );
  }

  // 4. One decode of every staged file, one at a time (AINV-8).
  for (const file of staged) {
    checkInterrupt();

    const seconds = durationOf.get(file.ref) as number;
    const decoded = await checkAudioFileDecodes(file.path, seconds, {
      ...options.preflight,
      ...(options.control === undefined ? {} : {control: options.control}),
    });

    if (!decoded.ok) {
      // A decode that was stopped by Ctrl+C is an interrupt, not an unusable file.
      checkInterrupt();
      throw unusable(
        file.name,
        describeAudioRefRoles(story, file.ref),
        decoded.detail,
      );
    }
  }

  return {audioDurations, audioSources};
};
```

### Task 5: `npm run video` renders audio (the CLI half of removing the interim block)

**Files:**
- Create: `stories/ci-audio.yaml`
- Modify: `scripts/renderStory.ts`, `tests/cli-local-assets.test.mjs`, `tests/render-interrupt.test.mjs`, `tests/cli-local-assets.e2e.mjs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 3 (`buildRenderArgs(..., {hasAudio})`), Task 4 (`prepareCliAudio`), `storyHasAudio`, `storyHasLocalAssetRefs` (stays image-only; it drives persistence v2).
- Produces: `RenderStoryDeps` gains `publicSourceDir?: string` (the directory copied into the staging directory and read for bundled audio, default `public`) and `audioPreflight?: AudioPreflightDeps`. For a Story with audio the props file is `{story, localAssetSources?, audioDurations, audioSources, bundledAudioPolicy: "explicit", audioArmed: true}` (`localAssetSources` only when the Story has image refs, image refs only); a Story without audio keeps exactly `{story}` (or `{story, localAssetSources}` for images). The staging gate is `storyHasLocalAssetRefs(story) || storyHasAudio(story)`, so a Story whose only local ref is audio, and a Story with only bundled audio, take the staged path. The AUDIO-001 CLI refusal (`AUDIO_RENDER_BLOCK_MESSAGE`) is gone from this file; the constant itself is removed in Task 13.

Edits to existing tests in this task: the two tests `refuses a Story with audio before writing props or starting Remotion (AUDIO-001 interim block)` and `still refuses a Story whose voice is a local audio ref before staging or starting Remotion` of `tests/cli-local-assets.test.mjs` are replaced by the new tests below (they assert the removed refusal).

```js
  const audioStoryYaml = (extra) =>

  const captureRun = (calls, inspect = async () => undefined) =>
    async (_executable, args) => {
      const propsArg = args.find((arg) => arg.startsWith("--props="));
      const publicArg = args.find((arg) => arg.startsWith("--public-dir="));
      const publicDir = publicArg?.slice("--public-dir=".length);

      calls.push({
        args,
        props: JSON.parse(await readFile(propsArg.slice(8), "utf8")),
        publicDir,
        extra: await inspect(publicDir),
      });
    };

    const {storyPath, tempBase, outputPath} = await setup(
      audioStoryYaml({music: ["music:", "  src: calm-loop"], scene: []}),
    );
    let spawned = 0;

    await assert.rejects(
      renderStory(storyPath, {
        tempBase,
        runRemotion: async () => {
          spawned += 1;
        },
      }),
      (error) => {
        assert.ok(error instanceof AudioRenderError);
        assert.match(error.message, /music: Bundled audio calm-loop is not available in this build\./u);

        return true;
      },
    );
    assert.equal(spawned, 0);
    assert.deepEqual(await readdir(tempBase), []);
    await assert.rejects(stat(outputPath), {code: "ENOENT"});
  });

  it("fails before spawning for a Story longer than 3,600 s with audio", async () => {
    const {storyPath, tempBase} = await setup(
      audioStoryYaml({music: noMusic, scene: sfxScene("ding")}).replace(
        "duration: 1",
        "duration: 3601",
      ),
    );
    let spawned = 0;

    await assert.rejects(
      renderStory(storyPath, {
        tempBase,
        runRemotion: async () => {
          spawned += 1;
        },
      }),
      /Stories with audio/u,
    );
    assert.equal(spawned, 0);
    assert.deepEqual(await readdir(tempBase), []);
  });

  it("refuses a truncated local MP3 from a real ffmpeg decode before spawning Remotion", async () => {
    const root = await makeRoot();
    const truncated = fixtureBytes("truncated.mp3");
    const {storyPath, tempBase} = await setup(
      audioStoryYaml({
        music: noMusic,
        scene: ["    voice:", `      src: ${audioRefOf(truncated)}`],
      }),
    );
    let spawned = 0;

    await put(root, "audio/voice.mp3", truncated);
    await assert.rejects(
      renderStory(storyPath, {
        tempBase,
        localAssetsRoot: root,
        runRemotion: async () => {
          spawned += 1;
        },
      }),
      /Audio file voice\.mp3 cannot be used for rendering\.[\s\S]*used by: scene 1 voice/u,
    );
    assert.equal(spawned, 0);
    assert.deepEqual(await readdir(tempBase), []);
  });

  it("decode failures (non-zero exit, timeout, 0.3 s short) refuse before Remotion and 0.2 s short renders", async () => {
    const run = async (script, timeoutMs) => {
      const {storyPath, tempBase, outputPath} = await setup(
        audioStoryYaml({music: noMusic, scene: sfxScene("ding")}),
      );
      let spawned = 0;
      let error = null;

      try {
        await renderStory(storyPath, {
          tempBase,
          runRemotion: async () => {
            spawned += 1;
          },
          audioPreflight: {
            spawn: fakeSpawn(script),
            ffmpeg: FAKE_FFMPEG,
            ...(timeoutMs === undefined ? {} : {timeoutMs}),
          },
        });
      } catch (caught) {
        error = caught;
      }

      assert.deepEqual(await readdir(tempBase), []);
      await rm(outputPath, {force: true});

      return {spawned, error};
    };
    const exit1 = await run((child) => child.emit("close", 1, null));
    const timeout = await run(() => undefined, 20);
    const short = await run(decodeSeconds(0.9));
    const nearlyWhole = await run(decodeSeconds(1.0));

    for (const refused of [exit1, timeout, short]) {
      assert.equal(refused.spawned, 0);
      assert.match(refused.error.message, /Audio file ding cannot be used for rendering\./u);
    }

    assert.equal(nearlyWhole.error, null);
    assert.equal(nearlyWhole.spawned, 1);
  });

  it("replacing public/audio/sfx/ding.wav after the preflight and before the spawn cannot reach the render", async () => {
    const source = await makeRoot();
    const original = await readFile(join(repoRoot, "public", "audio", "sfx", "ding.wav"));

    await cp(join(repoRoot, "public"), source, {recursive: true});

    const {storyPath, tempBase, outputPath} = await setup(
      audioStoryYaml({music: noMusic, scene: sfxScene("ding")}),
    );
    const calls = [];

    await renderStory(storyPath, {
      tempBase,
      publicSourceDir: source,
      runRemotion: async (executable, args) => {
        // The preflight is over: change the repository file now.
        await writeFile(join(source, "audio", "sfx", "ding.wav"), buildWav({seconds: 9}));
        await captureRun(calls, (publicDir) =>
          readFile(join(publicDir, "__local-assets", "bundled", "ding.wav")),
        )(executable, args);
      },
    });

    assert.equal(calls[0].props.audioSources.ding.path, "__local-assets/bundled/ding.wav");
    assert.equal(nodeSha(calls[0].extra), nodeSha(original));
    await rm(outputPath, {force: true});
  });

  it("removes the staged audio with the temporary directory when Remotion fails", async () => {
    const {storyPath, tempBase, outputPath} = await setup(
      audioStoryYaml({music: noMusic, scene: sfxScene("ding")}),
    );

    await assert.rejects(
      renderStory(storyPath, {
        tempBase,
        runRemotion: async () => {
          await mkdir(dirname(outputPath), {recursive: true});
          await writeFile(outputPath, "partial");
          throw new Error("Remotion render failed with exit code 1");
        },
      }),
      /Remotion render failed/u,
    );
    assert.deepEqual(await readdir(tempBase), []);
    await assert.rejects(stat(outputPath), {code: "ENOENT"});
  });
```

```js
          // An ffmpeg that decodes until the guard signals it, then exits.
          spawn: fakeSpawn((child) => {
            child.kill = (signal) => {
              kills.push(signal);
              queueMicrotask(() => child.emit("close", null, signal));

              return true;

            decoding.resolve();
          }),
        },
        interrupt: {signals, timers, platform: "linux"},
      }),
    );

    await decoding.promise;
    assert.equal((await readdir(tempBase)).length, 1);
    signals.emit("SIGINT");

    const error = await outcome;

    assert.ok(error instanceof RenderInterruptedError);
    assert.equal(error.exitCode, 130);
    assert.deepEqual(kills.slice(0, 1), ["SIGINT"]);
    assert.equal(remotionStarted, false);
    assert.deepEqual(await readdir(tempBase), []);
    assert.equal(await exists(outputPath), false);
    assert.deepEqual(timers.active(), []);
  });
```

```js
describe("CLI render with audio", () => {
  const audioStoryPath = "stories/ci-audio.yaml";
  const audioOutputPath = join(repoRoot, "output", "ci-audio.mp4");
  const silentStoryPath = "stories/friday-deploy.yaml";
  const silentOutputPath = join(repoRoot, "output", "friday-deploy.mp4");
  let workDirectory;
  let emptyRoot;
  let audioRender;
  let probe;

  const render = (story, localAssetsRoot) =>
    spawnSync(
      process.execPath,
      ["--experimental-strip-types", "scripts/render.ts", story],
      {
        cwd: repoRoot,
        env: {...process.env, TORA_LOCAL_ASSETS_ROOT: localAssetsRoot},
        encoding: "utf8",
        timeout: SPAWN_TIMEOUT_MS,
      },
    );
  const probeStreams = (path) => {
    const result = remotion(
      "ffprobe",
      "-v",
      "error",
      "-show_entries",
      "stream=codec_type,codec_name,profile,width,height,r_frame_rate,sample_rate,channels,bit_rate,duration,nb_frames",
      "-of",
      "json",
      path,
    );

    assert.equal(result.status, 0, result.stderr);

    return JSON.parse(result.stdout).streams;
  };

  before(async () => {
    workDirectory = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "tora-cli-audio-e2e-"));
    emptyRoot = join(workDirectory, "local-assets");
    await mkdir(emptyRoot);
    await rm(audioOutputPath, {force: true});
    audioRender = render(audioStoryPath, emptyRoot);
    probe = audioRender.status === 0 ? probeStreams(audioOutputPath) : [];
  });

  after(async () => {
    if (workDirectory) {
      await rm(workDirectory, {recursive: true, force: true});
    }

    await rm(audioOutputPath, {force: true});
    await rm(silentOutputPath, {force: true});
  });

  it("renders the Story with a bundled sfx from the staged copy and exits 0", async () => {
    assert.equal(audioRender.status, 0, audioRender.stderr || audioRender.stdout);
    assert.match(audioRender.stdout, /Rendered .*ci-audio\.mp4/u);
    assert.ok((await stat(audioOutputPath)).size > 0);
  });

  it("outputs h264 1080x1920 30 fps with 360 frames and one AAC-LC 48 kHz stereo stream at about 128 kb/s", () => {
    const video = probe.filter((stream) => stream.codec_type === "video");
    const audio = probe.filter((stream) => stream.codec_type === "audio");

    assert.equal(video.length, 1);
    assert.equal(video[0].codec_name, "h264");
    assert.deepEqual([video[0].width, video[0].height], [1080, 1920]);
    assert.equal(video[0].r_frame_rate, "30/1");
    assert.equal(Number(video[0].nb_frames), 360);

    assert.equal(audio.length, 1);
    assert.equal(audio[0].codec_name, "aac");
    // The repository ffprobe prints the AAC profile as its number (1 = LC).
    assert.ok(["LC", "1"].includes(audio[0].profile), `profile ${audio[0].profile}`);
    assert.equal(Number(audio[0].sample_rate), 48_000);
    assert.equal(audio[0].channels, 2);

    const bitRate = Number(audio[0].bit_rate);

    assert.ok(bitRate >= 115_200 && bitRate <= 140_800, `bit rate ${bitRate}`);

    // 1 / fps plus the 60 ms the spec allows for the encoder's priming.
    const drift = Math.abs(Number(audio[0].duration) - Number(video[0].duration));

    assert.ok(drift <= 1 / 30 + 0.06, `audio ${audio[0].duration} s, video ${video[0].duration} s`);
  });

  it("plays the ding at the scene start and nothing after it (Goertzel on the decoded MP4)", async () => {
    const samples = await decodeMp4AudioMono(audioOutputPath);
    const fundamental = goertzelWindows(samples, 880, 100);
    const partial = goertzelWindows(samples, 1760, 100);

    // Positive control: the sfx is clearly there in the first 300 ms...
    for (const windows of [fundamental, partial]) {
      assert.ok(windows[0] > 0.02 && windows[1] > 0.02, windows.slice(0, 3).join(" "));
    }

    // ...it decays, and nothing sounds from 2 s on (the Story has no other audio).
    for (const windows of [fundamental, partial]) {
      assert.ok(
        windows.slice(20).every((amplitude) => amplitude < 0.005),
        `after 2 s: ${Math.max(...windows.slice(20))}`,
      );
    }
  });

  it("fails before spawning Remotion on a truncated local voice and leaves no MP4", async () => {
    const root = join(workDirectory, "truncated-root");
    const truncated = await readFile(
      join(repoRoot, "tests", "fixtures", "local-audio", "truncated.mp3"),
    );
    const slug = "ci-audio-truncated";
    const story = join(workDirectory, `${slug}.yaml`);

    await mkdir(join(root, "audio"), {recursive: true});
    await cp(
      join(repoRoot, "tests", "fixtures", "local-audio", "truncated.mp3"),
      join(root, "audio", "voice.mp3"),
    );
    await writeFile(
      story,
      [
        'title: "CI truncated voice"',
        "scenes:",
        "  - type: intro",
        "    pose: formal",
        "    background: office",
        '    text: "Truncated."',
        "    voice:",
        `      src: local:audio:sha256:${sha256(truncated)}`,
        "    duration: auto",
        "",
      ].join("\n"),
      "utf8",
    );

    const result = render(story, root);

    assert.equal(result.status, 1);
    assert.match(result.stderr, /Audio file voice\.mp3 cannot be used for rendering/u);
    assert.doesNotMatch(result.stdout, /Rendered \d+\/\d+/u);
    await assert.rejects(stat(join(repoRoot, "output", `${slug}.mp4`)), {code: "ENOENT"});
  });

  it("a Story without audio (friday-deploy.yaml) still renders video-only", async () => {
    await rm(silentOutputPath, {force: true});

    const result = render(silentStoryPath, emptyRoot);

    assert.equal(result.status, 0, result.stderr || result.stdout);

    const streams = probeStreams(silentOutputPath);

    assert.deepEqual(streams.map((stream) => stream.codec_type), ["video"]);
    assert.equal(Number(streams[0].nb_frames), 360);
  });
});
```

*New:*

```ts
      // A signal while the directory was being created: skip the staging.
      guard.throwIfInterrupted();

      const hasAudio = storyHasAudio(story);
      const publicSourceDir = deps.publicSourceDir ?? "public";
      let props: Record<string, unknown> = {story};
      let publicDir: string | undefined;

      // A Story without local refs and without audio never reaches this
      // branch, so the root is not required for it and its command line and
      // props are exactly the v0.3 ones (AINV-6). Audio needs the staging
      // directory even when it is all bundled: Remotion reads the staged copy.
      if (storyHasLocalAssetRefs(story) || hasAudio) {
        publicDir = join(temporaryDirectory, "public");
        await cp(publicSourceDir, publicDir, {recursive: true});

        const localNames = new Map<string, string>();
        const staged = await stageLocalAssetsForStory(story, {
          root: deps.localAssetsRoot ?? getLocalAssetsRoot(),
          publicDir,
          onStaged: (ref, _stagedPath, relativePath) => {
            localNames.set(ref, relativePath);
          },
        });

        if (storyHasLocalAssetRefs(story)) {
          // Images only: the audio sources travel in `audioSources`.
          props = {
            story,
            localAssetSources: Object.fromEntries(
              Object.entries(staged.sources).filter(
                ([ref]) => !isLocalAudioRef(ref),
              ),
            ) as LocalAssetSourceMap,
          };
        }

        if (hasAudio) {
          guard.throwIfInterrupted();

          const audio = await prepareCliAudio(story, {
            publicDir,
            localSources: staged.sources,
            localNames,
            publicSourceDir,
            checkInterrupt: () => guard.throwIfInterrupted(),
            control: guard,
            preflight: deps.audioPreflight,
          });

          props = {
            ...props,
            audioDurations: audio.audioDurations,
            audioSources: audio.audioSources,
            bundledAudioPolicy: "explicit",
            audioArmed: true,
          };
        }
      }

      await writeFile(propsPath, JSON.stringify(props), "utf8");
```

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/audio/readiness.ts`, `src/localAssets/readiness.ts`, `src/web/localAssetState.ts`, `tests/local-asset-readiness.test.mjs`, `tests/local-asset-state.test.mjs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `splitUsagesByCategory`, `LocalAssetBudgetResult`, `isAudioPayload` (`src/localAssets/readiness.ts`), `MAX_BROWSER_STORY_AUDIO_REFS`, `MAX_BROWSER_STORY_AUDIO_BYTES`.
- Produces:
  - `src/audio/readiness.ts`: `evaluateAudioBudget(usages: readonly LocalAudioUsage[], metadataByDigest: ReadonlyMap<string, PayloadMetadata>): LocalAssetBudgetResult` (audio usages only; `totalPixels` is 0; non-audio metadata fails closed with `"metadata"`); `describeAudioBudgetFailure(result): string` (`This Story uses more than 64 audio files.` / `This Story's audio is larger than 256 MiB.` / `An audio file's stored details do not match an audio file.`).
  - `evaluateLocalAssetBudget` also fails closed (`"metadata"`) on image metadata without finite `width`/`height`.
  - `resolveStoryLocalAssets` evaluates both budgets (refs before any storage read, bytes from metadata before any blob read); when both fail the `over-budget` message joins both sentences and `budget` is the image one.

```js
// --- AUDIO-005: the audio budget ------------------------------------------------

const MIB = 1024 * 1024;

const audioUsageOf = (n) => ({
  ref: audioRefOf(n),
  category: "audio",
  digest: digestOf(n),
  sceneIndexes: [0],
  roles: ["voice"],
});

const audioMetadata = (byteSize) => ({
  kind: "audio",
  mimeType: "audio/wav",
  byteSize,
  container: "wave",
  codec: "pcm",
  durationSeconds: 10,
  sampleRate: 48_000,
  channels: 2,
});

test("the audio budget passes at 64 refs and fails at 65 with its own sentence", () => {
  const at = (count) =>
    evaluateAudioBudget(
      Array.from({length: count}, (_, i) => audioUsageOf(i + 1)),
      new Map(),
    );

  assert.equal(MAX_BROWSER_STORY_AUDIO_REFS, 64);
  assert.deepEqual(at(64), {ok: true, refCount: 64, totalBytes: 0, totalPixels: 0});

  const over = at(65);

  assert.equal(over.ok, false);
  assert.deepEqual(over.exceeded, ["refs"]);
  assert.equal(describeAudioBudgetFailure(over), "This Story uses more than 64 audio files.");
});

test("the audio budget sums bytes once per digest: 256 MiB passes, one byte more fails", () => {
  const usages = [1, 2, 3, 4].map(audioUsageOf);
  const at = (last) =>
    evaluateAudioBudget(
      [...usages, audioUsageOf(1)],
      new Map([
        [digestOf(1), audioMetadata(64 * MIB)],
        [digestOf(2), audioMetadata(64 * MIB)],
        [digestOf(3), audioMetadata(64 * MIB)],
        [digestOf(4), audioMetadata(last)],
      ]),
    );

  assert.equal(MAX_BROWSER_STORY_AUDIO_BYTES, 256 * MIB);
  assert.equal(at(64 * MIB).ok, true);
  assert.equal(at(64 * MIB).totalBytes, 256 * MIB);

  const over = at(64 * MIB + 1);

  assert.equal(over.ok, false);
  assert.deepEqual(over.exceeded, ["bytes"]);
  assert.equal(describeAudioBudgetFailure(over), "This Story's audio is larger than 256 MiB.");
});

test("each budget counts only its own refs and bytes", () => {
  const imageUsages = Array.from({length: 64}, (_, i) => usageOf(i + 1));
  const audioUsages = Array.from({length: 64}, (_, i) => audioUsageOf(500 + i));
  const imageMeta = new Map(imageUsages.map((u) => [u.digest, metadata(4 * MIB, 1000, 1000)]));
  const audioMeta = new Map(audioUsages.map((u) => [u.digest, audioMetadata(4 * MIB)]));
  const story = withScenes(
    storyOf(imageUsages.map((u) => [u.ref, "office"])),
    audioUsages.map((u) => ({voice: {src: u.ref}})),
  );
  const split = splitUsagesByCategory(collectStoryLocalAssetUsages(story));
  const all = new Map([...imageMeta, ...audioMeta]);

  // 64 + 64 refs, 256 MiB + 256 MiB: each budget is exactly at its limit.
  assert.deepEqual(evaluateLocalAssetBudget(split.image, all), {
    ok: true,
    refCount: 64,
    totalBytes: 256 * MIB,
    totalPixels: 64_000_000,
  });
  assert.deepEqual(evaluateAudioBudget(split.audio, all), {
    ok: true,
    refCount: 64,
    totalBytes: 256 * MIB,
    totalPixels: 0,
  });

  // One more of either kind fails only that kind.
  assert.equal(evaluateLocalAssetBudget([...split.image, usageOf(99)], all).ok, false);
  assert.equal(evaluateAudioBudget(split.audio, all).ok, true);
  assert.equal(evaluateAudioBudget([...split.audio, audioUsageOf(999)], all).ok, false);
  assert.equal(evaluateLocalAssetBudget(split.image, all).ok, true);
});

  assert.equal(imageResult.ok, false);

test("image metadata without finite dimensions fails closed instead of making the pixel total NaN", () => {
  for (const bad of [
    {...metadata(1000), width: undefined},
    {...metadata(1000), height: Number.NaN},
    {...metadata(1000), width: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY},
  ]) {
    const result = evaluateLocalAssetBudget([usageOf(1)], new Map([[digestOf(1), bad]]));

    assert.equal(result.ok, false);
    assert.deepEqual(result.exceeded, ["metadata"]);
    assert.equal(Number.isNaN(result.totalPixels), false);
  }
});
```

```ts
/** The one text for a source that failed a render preflight (metadata or decode). */
export const audioFileUnusableMessage = (name: string): string =>
  `Audio file ${name} cannot be used for rendering.`;

/**
 * The audio budget of a Story in the browser (D-17), separate from the image
 * budget: it receives audio usages and audio metadata only.
 * refCount = number of usages (missing ones included); bytes are summed once
 * per distinct digest that has metadata. A value equal to its limit passes.
 * Metadata that is not audio fails closed ("metadata"), never as a number.
 */
export const evaluateAudioBudget = (
  usages: readonly LocalAudioUsage[],
  metadataByDigest: ReadonlyMap<string, PayloadMetadata>,
): LocalAssetBudgetResult => {
  const refCount = usages.length;
  let totalBytes = 0;
  let nonAudioMetadata = false;

  for (const digest of new Set(usages.map((usage) => usage.digest))) {
    const metadata = metadataByDigest.get(digest);

    if (metadata === undefined) {
      continue;
    }

    if (!isAudioPayload(metadata) || !Number.isFinite(metadata.byteSize)) {
      nonAudioMetadata = true;
      continue;
    }

    totalBytes += metadata.byteSize;
  }

  const exceeded: Array<"refs" | "bytes" | "pixels" | "metadata"> = [];

  if (refCount > MAX_BROWSER_STORY_AUDIO_REFS) {
    exceeded.push("refs");
  }

  if (totalBytes > MAX_BROWSER_STORY_AUDIO_BYTES) {
    exceeded.push("bytes");
  }

  if (nonAudioMetadata) {
    exceeded.push("metadata");
  }

  // There is no pixel budget for audio: the shared result type carries a 0.
  return exceeded.length === 0
    ? {ok: true, refCount, totalBytes, totalPixels: 0}
    : {ok: false, refCount, totalBytes, totalPixels: 0, exceeded};
};

export const describeAudioBudgetFailure = (
  result: Extract<LocalAssetBudgetResult, {ok: false}>,
): string => {
  const sentences: string[] = [];

  for (const limit of result.exceeded) {
    if (limit === "refs") {
      sentences.push(
        `This Story uses more than ${MAX_BROWSER_STORY_AUDIO_REFS} audio files.`,
      );
    } else if (limit === "bytes") {
      sentences.push(
        `This Story's audio is larger than ${MAX_BROWSER_STORY_AUDIO_BYTES / (1024 * 1024)} MiB.`,
      );
    } else if (limit === "metadata") {
      sentences.push("An audio file's stored details do not match an audio file.");
    }
  }

  return sentences.join(" ");
};
```

```ts
/**
 * The over-budget state when the image budget or the audio budget failed
 * (each budget counts its own refs and bytes), else null. Both messages are
 * shown when both failed; `budget` is the first failure (images first).
 */
const overBudgetState = (
  usages: readonly LocalAssetUsage[],
  image: LocalAssetBudgetResult,
  audio: LocalAssetBudgetResult,
): Extract<StoryLocalAssetState, {kind: "over-budget"}> | null => {
  if (image.ok && audio.ok) {
    return null;
  }

  const messages = [
    ...(image.ok ? [] : [describeBudgetFailure(image)]),
    ...(audio.ok ? [] : [describeAudioBudgetFailure(audio)]),
  ];

  return {
    kind: "over-budget",
    usages: [...usages],
    budget: (image.ok ? audio : image) as Extract<
      LocalAssetBudgetResult,
      {ok: false}
    >,
    message: messages.join(" "),
  };
};
```

```ts
    const digests = new Set(categoryUsages.map((usage) => usage.digest));

    return new Map(
      [...metadataByDigest].filter(
        ([digest, metadata]) =>
          digests.has(digest) && payloadKindOf(metadata) === kind,
      ),
    );
  };
  const budgetFailure = overBudgetState(
    usages,
    evaluateLocalAssetBudget(imageUsages, metadataOfKind("image", imageUsages)),
    evaluateAudioBudget(audioUsages, metadataOfKind("audio", audioUsages)),
  );

  if (budgetFailure !== null) {
    return budgetFailure;
  }

  // 6. Sequential verification; one failed digest does not stop the others.
```

### Task 7: Render readiness before the click

**Files:**
- Create: `src/web/audioRenderReadiness.ts`, `tests/audio-render-readiness.test.mjs`
- Modify: `src/audio/readiness.ts` (one sentence)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `compileTimeline`, `compileAudioPlan`, `storyAudioRefs`, `storyHasAudio`, `shortAssetId`, `MAX_BROWSER_TOTAL_FRAMES`, `StoryLocalAssetState` (type only).
- Produces:
  - `src/audio/readiness.ts`: `storyTooLongWithVoicesMessage(seconds: number): string`.
  - `src/web/audioRenderReadiness.ts`: `AUDIO_LOADING_MESSAGE`, `AUDIO_FETCH_FAILED_MESSAGE`; `BundledAudioStateLike` (structural: `loading | retrying{attempt} | ready | fetch-failed{message} | unusable{message}`); `evaluateAudioRenderReadiness(input: {story: Story; audioDurations: AudioDurationMap; localState: StoryLocalAssetState; bundledStates: ReadonlyMap<BundledAudio, BundledAudioStateLike>}): {block: string | null; plan: AudioPlan; audioDurations: AudioDurationMap}` (the returned map is the page's map plus the durations of the Story's verified local audio, which are the ones the render uses). Local ref: `ready` when the local-asset state has it ready and its verified payload carries a duration (so a failed read of the page's own duration map never leaves it loading), `pending` while resolving, `missing` otherwise (including a ready ref without a verified payload); bundled id: `ready` -> ready, `loading`/`retrying`/`fetch-failed`/absent -> pending, `unusable` -> missing. The block is, in this order: `Audio file <id|short id> cannot be used for rendering.`, `Audio could not be loaded. Retry it in the editor.`, `Audio is still loading.`, `This Story is longer than 300 s with its voices (N s).` (the exact `compileTimeline(...).durationInFrames` over 9,000 frames).
- The acceptance policy (`evaluateBrowserStoryPolicy`, AUDIO-001) is not changed: it counts each `auto` scene at `AUTO_DURATION_MIN_FRAMES` and must not depend on asynchronous asset state; the tests below pin both checks side by side. That a stored project with `auto` scenes loads as active before My assets opens is already pinned by `tests/persistence-v3.test.mjs` (`restores a stored project with auto scenes before any duration is known`).

```js
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import {describe, it} from "node:test";
import {
  AUDIO_FETCH_FAILED_MESSAGE,
  AUDIO_LOADING_MESSAGE,
  evaluateAudioRenderReadiness,
} from "../src/web/audioRenderReadiness.ts";
import {evaluateBrowserStoryPolicy, MAX_BROWSER_TOTAL_FRAMES} from "../src/web/browserPolicy.ts";
import {storyAudioDurations} from "../src/audio/storyAudio.ts";

const VOICE = `local:audio:sha256:${"a".repeat(64)}`;
const scene = (overrides = {}) => ({
  type: "dialogue",
  pose: "formal",
  background: "office",
  text: "Hi",
  duration: 3,
  ...overrides,
});
const dingStory = {title: "Ding", scenes: [scene({sfx: {src: "ding"}})]};
const states = (entries) => new Map(Object.entries(entries));
const NONE = {kind: "none"};
/** `durations` are the verified durations of the ready refs (a ready ref without one has no verified payload). */
const resolvedLocal = (refs, durations = {}) => ({
  kind: "resolved",
  refs: refs.map(([ref, status]) => ({usage: {ref, category: "audio"}, status, detail: null})),
  verified: new Map(
    refs
      .filter(([ref, status]) => status === "ready" && durations[ref] !== undefined)
      .map(([ref]) => [
        ref.slice(ref.length - 64),
        {
          digest: ref.slice(ref.length - 64),
          kind: "audio",
          mimeType: "audio/wav",
          byteSize: 1000,
          container: "wave",
          codec: "pcm",
          durationSeconds: durations[ref],
          sampleRate: 48_000,
          channels: 2,
        },
      ]),
  ),
  blobs: new Map(),
  allReady: refs.every(([, status]) => status === "ready"),
  failureMessage: null,
});
const evaluate = (story, bundled = {}, options = {}) =>
  evaluateAudioRenderReadiness({
    story,
    // The page's own map, which a failed duration read leaves without the local durations.
    audioDurations: options.audioDurations ?? storyAudioDurations(story),
    localState: options.localState ?? NONE,
    bundledStates: states(bundled),
  });
const issueKinds = (readiness) => readiness.plan.issues.map((issue) => issue.kind);

describe("readiness over the bundled-audio store's states", () => {
  it("a Story without audio is never blocked and compiles an empty plan", () => {
    const readiness = evaluate({title: "Silent", scenes: [scene()]});

    assert.equal(readiness.block, null);
    assert.deepEqual(readiness.plan.tracks, []);
    assert.deepEqual(readiness.plan.issues, []);
  });

  for (const [title, state] of [
    ["not requested yet (no entry)", undefined],
    ["loading", {kind: "loading"}],
    ["retrying", {kind: "retrying", attempt: 2}],
  ]) {
    it(`a bundled id that is ${title} has no track, a pending-audio issue and blocks with "Audio is still loading."`, () => {
      const readiness = evaluate(dingStory, state === undefined ? {} : {ding: state});

      assert.equal(readiness.block, "Audio is still loading.");
      assert.equal(AUDIO_LOADING_MESSAGE, "Audio is still loading.");
      assert.deepEqual(readiness.plan.tracks, []);
      assert.deepEqual(issueKinds(readiness), ["pending-audio"]);
    });
  }

  it("fetch-failed blocks with the retry sentence (still a pending-audio issue: the person can retry)", () => {
    const readiness = evaluate(dingStory, {ding: {kind: "fetch-failed", message: "Network"}});

    assert.equal(readiness.block, "Audio could not be loaded. Retry it in the editor.");
    assert.equal(AUDIO_FETCH_FAILED_MESSAGE, readiness.block);
    assert.deepEqual(issueKinds(readiness), ["pending-audio"]);
  });

  it("unusable blocks terminally with missing-audio and the id", () => {
    const readiness = evaluate(dingStory, {ding: {kind: "unusable", message: "bad"}});

    assert.equal(readiness.block, "Audio file ding cannot be used for rendering.");
    assert.deepEqual(readiness.plan.tracks, []);
    assert.deepEqual(issueKinds(readiness), ["missing-audio"]);
  });

  it("the same Story enables by itself when the store reports ready: track present, no block", () => {
    const blocked = evaluate(dingStory, {ding: {kind: "loading"}});
    const ready = evaluate(dingStory, {ding: {kind: "ready"}});

    assert.notEqual(blocked.block, null);
    assert.equal(ready.block, null);
    assert.deepEqual(ready.plan.issues, []);
    assert.deepEqual(ready.plan.tracks.map((track) => track.key), ["sfx-0"]);
  });

  it("reports a terminal problem before a failed fetch, a failed fetch before loading", () => {
    const story = {
      title: "Three",
      scenes: [scene({sfx: {src: "whoosh"}}), scene({sfx: {src: "boom"}}), scene({sfx: {src: "ding"}})],
    };

    assert.equal(
      evaluate(story, {
        whoosh: {kind: "loading"},
        boom: {kind: "fetch-failed", message: "x"},
        ding: {kind: "unusable", message: "y"},
      }).block,
      "Audio file ding cannot be used for rendering.",
    );
    assert.equal(
      evaluate(story, {
        whoosh: {kind: "loading"},
        boom: {kind: "fetch-failed", message: "x"},
        ding: {kind: "ready"},
      }).block,
      AUDIO_FETCH_FAILED_MESSAGE,
    );
  });
});

describe("readiness over the page's local-asset state", () => {
  const voiceStory = {title: "Voice", scenes: [scene({voice: {src: VOICE}})]};

  it("a verified local voice is ready with the duration its verified payload carries, even when the page's own duration map has no entry for it (a failed read never leaves 'Audio is still loading.')", () => {
    const readiness = evaluate(voiceStory, {}, {

    const readiness = evaluate(voiceStory, {}, {localState: resolvedLocal([[VOICE, "ready"]])});

    assert.match(readiness.block, /^Audio file aaaa…aaaa cannot be used for rendering.$/u);
    assert.deepEqual(issueKinds(readiness), ["missing-audio"]);
  });

  it("a local voice that is still resolving is loading; a missing, corrupt or unavailable one is terminal", () => {
    assert.equal(
      evaluate(voiceStory, {}, {localState: {kind: "pending", usages: []}}).block,

    );

    for (const status of ["missing", "corrupt", "unavailable"]) {

        localState: resolvedLocal([[VOICE, status]], {[VOICE]: 2.2}),
      });

    const story = {title: "Both", scenes: [scene({voice: {src: VOICE}, sfx: {src: "ding"}})]};
    const options = {localState: resolvedLocal([[VOICE, "ready"]], {[VOICE]: 2.2})};

});

describe("two different frame checks, on purpose", () => {
  const autoScene = () =>
    scene({voice: {src: "ding"}, duration: "auto"});
  const autoStory = (count) => ({
    title: "Auto",
    scenes: Array.from({length: count}, autoScene),
  });

  it("the acceptance policy counts each auto scene at its lower bound and accepts 101 of them with an empty map", () => {
    const story = autoStory(101);
    const policy = evaluateBrowserStoryPolicy(story);

    assert.equal(policy.eligible, true);
    // 101 x 16 frames, not 101 x 90 fallback frames (which would be over the cap).
    assert.equal(policy.totalFrames, 101 * 16);
    assert.ok(101 * 90 > MAX_BROWSER_TOTAL_FRAMES);
  });

  it("readiness checks the exact total: 101 bundled ding voices (51 frames each) fit, 101 three-second local voices do not", () => {
    assert.equal(
      evaluate(autoStory(101), {ding: {kind: "ready"}}).block,
      null,
    );

    const longStory = {
      title: "Long",
      scenes: Array.from({length: 101}, () => scene({voice: {src: VOICE}, duration: "auto"})),
    };
    const readiness = evaluate(longStory, {}, {
      localState: resolvedLocal([[VOICE, "ready"]], {[VOICE]: 3}),
    });

    // ceil((3 + 0.5) x 30) = 105 frames x 101 = 10,605 frames = 353.5 s.
    assert.equal(readiness.block, "This Story is longer than 300 s with its voices (354 s).");
  });

  it("exactly 9,000 frames (300 s) passes and one more frame blocks", () => {
    // Scenes of 3 s fixed + 1 auto scene: the auto scene has ceil((s + 0.5) x 30) frames.
    // 99 x 90 = 8,910 frames; the auto scene gets the remaining 90 (s = 2.5) or 91 (s = 2.5 + 1/30).
    const edgeStory = {
      title: "Edge",
      scenes: [
        ...Array.from({length: 99}, () => scene()),
        scene({voice: {src: VOICE}, duration: "auto"}),
      ],
    };
    const run = (seconds) =>
      evaluate(edgeStory, {}, {
        localState: resolvedLocal([[VOICE, "ready"]], {[VOICE]: seconds}),
      });

    assert.equal(run(2.5).block, null);
    assert.equal(
      run(2.5 + 1 / 30).block,
      "This Story is longer than 300 s with its voices (301 s).",
    );
  });

  it("a loading source is reported before the length, because a fallback scene would make the total meaningless", () => {
    const story = {
      title: "Long and loading",
      scenes: Array.from({length: 101}, () => scene({voice: {src: "ding"}, duration: "auto"})),
    };

    assert.equal(evaluate(story, {ding: {kind: "loading"}}).block, AUDIO_LOADING_MESSAGE);
  });
});
```

```ts
export const bundledAudioMismatchMessage = (id: string): string =>
  `Bundled audio ${id} does not match the catalog.`;

/**
 * Render readiness (exact total, not the acceptance lower bound): the text of the
 * block when `compileTimeline(...)` is longer than the browser's 300 s.
 * `seconds` is the exact total, rounded up to whole seconds.
 */
export const storyTooLongWithVoicesMessage = (seconds: number): string =>
  `This Story is longer than 300 s with its voices (${seconds} s).`;

/** The one text for a source that failed a render preflight (metadata or decode). */
```

Create `src/web/audioRenderReadiness.ts`:

```ts
// Render readiness of a Story's audio in the browser, evaluated before the
// Render MP4 click (AUDIO-005). It is a pure function of the Story, the page's
// duration map, the page's local-asset state and the bundled-audio store's
// snapshot, so the button enables by itself when the last pending id becomes
// ready. It never uses the render's own source map: that map only exists
// inside the transaction, after the click.

import {
  isBundledAudio,
  type AudioDurationMap,
  type AudioRef,
  type BundledAudio,
} from "../audio/catalog.ts";
import {compileAudioPlan, type AudioPlan} from "../audio/plan.ts";
import {
  audioFileUnusableMessage,
  storyTooLongWithVoicesMessage,
} from "../audio/readiness.ts";
import {storyAudioRefs, storyHasAudio} from "../audio/storyAudio.ts";
import {isAudioPayload} from "../localAssets/readiness.ts";
import {isLocalAudioRef} from "../localAssets/refs.ts";
import {shortAssetId} from "../localAssets/sources.ts";
import {compileTimeline} from "../story/timeline.ts";
import type {Story} from "../story/types.ts";
import {VIDEO_FPS} from "../videoConfig.ts";
import {MAX_BROWSER_TOTAL_FRAMES} from "./browserPolicy.ts";
import type {StoryLocalAssetState} from "./localAssetState.ts";

export const AUDIO_LOADING_MESSAGE = "Audio is still loading.";
export const AUDIO_FETCH_FAILED_MESSAGE =
  "Audio could not be loaded. Retry it in the editor.";

/**
 * The shape of one entry of the bundled-audio store (`src/web/audio/bundledAudio.ts`,
 * AUDIO-004). Declared here structurally so this module does not depend on the
 * store's code, and so it can run in Node tests.
 */
export type BundledAudioStateLike =
  | {kind: "loading"}
  | {kind: "retrying"; attempt: number}
  | {kind: "ready"}
  | {kind: "fetch-failed"; message: string}
  | {kind: "unusable"; message: string};

export type AudioRefReadiness = "ready" | "pending" | "missing";

export type AudioRenderReadiness = {
  /** Why Render MP4 is blocked by the audio, or null. */
  block: string | null;
  /** The plan compiled with the readiness source states (tracks only for ready sources). */
  plan: AudioPlan;
  /**
   * The durations the plan was compiled with: the page's map plus the durations
   * of the verified local audio, which are the ones the render will use.
   */
  audioDurations: AudioDurationMap;
};

const bundledReadiness = (
  state: BundledAudioStateLike | undefined,
): AudioRefReadiness => {
  switch (state?.kind) {
    case "ready":
      return "ready";
    case "unusable":
      return "missing";
    default:
      // Not requested yet, loading, retrying or waiting for a manual retry.
      return "pending";
  }
};

const localReadiness = (
  ref: AudioRef,
  localState: StoryLocalAssetState,
  audioDurations: AudioDurationMap,
): AudioRefReadiness => {
  if (localState.kind !== "resolved") {
    // Not resolved yet (pending), over budget or no local refs: never ready.
    return "pending";
  }

  const entry = localState.refs.find((candidate) => candidate.usage.ref === ref);

  if (entry === undefined || entry.status !== "ready") {
    return "missing";
  }

  // Verified: its duration is known (a verified payload carries it). A verified
  // ref without any duration is inconsistent data, never "still loading".
  return audioDurations[ref] === undefined ? "missing" : "ready";
};

/**
 * The page's map plus the durations of the Story's verified local audio. A
 * verified payload is exactly what the render will use, and reading it here
 * means a failed read of the page's own duration map can never leave the
 * Story at "Audio is still loading." forever.
 */
const durationsWithVerified = (
  story: Story,
  audioDurations: AudioDurationMap,
  localState: StoryLocalAssetState,
): AudioDurationMap => {
  if (localState.kind !== "resolved") {
    return audioDurations;
  }

  const verifiedDurations: Partial<Record<AudioRef, number>> = {};

  for (const ref of storyAudioRefs(story)) {
    if (!isLocalAudioRef(ref)) {
      continue;
    }

    const verified = localState.verified.get(ref.slice(ref.length - 64));

    if (
      verified !== undefined &&
      isAudioPayload(verified) &&
      Number.isFinite(verified.durationSeconds) &&
      verified.durationSeconds > 0
    ) {
      verifiedDurations[ref] = verified.durationSeconds;
    }
  }

  return {...audioDurations, ...verifiedDurations};
};

type ReadinessInput = {
  story: Story;
  /** The page's one duration map (`useStoryAudioDurations`). */
  audioDurations: AudioDurationMap;
  localState: StoryLocalAssetState;
  bundledStates: ReadonlyMap<BundledAudio, BundledAudioStateLike>;
};

const evaluateWithDurations = (
  input: ReadinessInput,
): Omit<AudioRenderReadiness, "audioDurations"> => {
  const {story, audioDurations, localState, bundledStates} = input;
  const timeline = compileTimeline(story, VIDEO_FPS, audioDurations);
  const readinessOf = (ref: AudioRef): AudioRefReadiness =>
    isBundledAudio(ref)
      ? bundledReadiness(bundledStates.get(ref))
      : localReadiness(ref, localState, audioDurations);
  const plan = compileAudioPlan({
    story,
    timeline,
    fps: VIDEO_FPS,
    audioDurations,
    sourceState: readinessOf,
  });

  if (!storyHasAudio(story)) {
    return {block: null, plan};
  }

  // Terminal problems first (the person must act), then a failed fetch, then
  // plain loading, and only then the length: a fallback scene would make the
  // exact total meaningless while durations are still unknown.
  let unusable: string | null = null;
  let fetchFailed = false;
  let loading = false;

  for (const ref of storyAudioRefs(story)) {
    const readiness = readinessOf(ref);

    if (readiness === "missing") {
      unusable ??= audioFileUnusableMessage(
        isBundledAudio(ref) ? ref : shortAssetId(ref),
      );
    } else if (readiness === "pending") {
      if (isBundledAudio(ref) && bundledStates.get(ref)?.kind === "fetch-failed") {
        fetchFailed = true;
      } else {
        loading = true;
      }
    }
  }

  if (unusable !== null) {
    return {block: unusable, plan};
  }

  if (fetchFailed) {
    return {block: AUDIO_FETCH_FAILED_MESSAGE, plan};
  }

  if (loading) {
    return {block: AUDIO_LOADING_MESSAGE, plan};
  }

  if (timeline.durationInFrames > MAX_BROWSER_TOTAL_FRAMES) {
    return {
      block: storyTooLongWithVoicesMessage(
        Math.ceil(timeline.durationInFrames / VIDEO_FPS),
      ),
      plan,
    };
  }

  return {block: null, plan};
};

export const evaluateAudioRenderReadiness = (
  input: ReadinessInput,
): AudioRenderReadiness => {
  const audioDurations = storyHasAudio(input.story)
    ? durationsWithVerified(input.story, input.audioDurations, input.localState)
    : input.audioDurations;

  return {
    ...evaluateWithDurations({...input, audioDurations}),
    audioDurations,
  };
};
```

Create `tests/audio-capability.test.mjs`:

```js
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import {readFile} from "node:fs/promises";
import {describe, it} from "node:test";
import {exampleStory} from "../src/story/exampleStory.ts";
import {
  AUDIO_UNSUPPORTED_MESSAGE,
  checkBrowserRenderCapability,
  startBrowserRenderTransaction,
} from "../src/web/browserRender.ts";
import {createRenderCapabilityCache} from "../src/web/renderCapabilityCache.ts";

describe("AUDIO-005 browser capability with audio (D-13)", () => {
  const withLocks = {getLocks: () => ({query: async () => ({held: [], pending: []})})};
  const result = (overrides = {}) => ({
    canRender: true,
    issues: [],
    resolvedVideoCodec: "h264",
    resolvedAudioCodec: "aac",
    resolvedOutputTarget: "web-fs",
    ...overrides,
  });
  const audioIssue = {
    type: "audio-codec-unsupported",
    message: 'Audio codec "aac" cannot be encoded by this browser.',
    severity: "error",
  };
  const videoIssue = {
    type: "video-codec-unsupported",
    message: 'Video codec "h264" cannot be encoded by this browser',
    severity: "error",
  };

  it("asks for unmuted AAC at the output bitrate when the Story has audio", async () => {
    let seen = null;
    const capability = await checkBrowserRenderCapability({
      ...withLocks,
      hasAudio: true,
      canRender: async (options) => {
        seen = options;

        return result();
      },
    });

    assert.deepEqual(capability, {kind: "ready"});
    assert.deepEqual(seen, {
      container: "mp4",
      videoCodec: "h264",
      width: 1080,
      height: 1920,
      muted: false,
      audioCodec: "aac",
      audioBitrate: 128_000,
    });
  });

  it("keeps the v0.3 muted probe for a silent Story, with hasAudio absent or false", async () => {
    const seen = [];
    const canRender = async (options) => {
      seen.push(options);

      return result({resolvedAudioCodec: null});
    };

    await checkBrowserRenderCapability({...withLocks, canRender});
    await checkBrowserRenderCapability({...withLocks, canRender, hasAudio: false});

    const silent = {container: "mp4", videoCodec: "h264", width: 1080, height: 1920, muted: true};

    assert.deepEqual(seen, [silent, silent]);
  });

  it("blocks with the CLI sentence when only the audio codec cannot be encoded", async () => {
    const capability = await checkBrowserRenderCapability({
      ...withLocks,
      hasAudio: true,
      canRender: async () => result({canRender: false, issues: [audioIssue], resolvedAudioCodec: null}),
    });

    assert.deepEqual(capability, {
      kind: "audio-unsupported",
      message: "This browser cannot encode audio. Use the local CLI: npm run video -- <story.yaml>",
    });
    assert.equal(capability.message, AUDIO_UNSUPPORTED_MESSAGE);
  });

  it("reports a video problem as the usual unsupported message, even when the audio is also missing", async () => {
    const capability = await checkBrowserRenderCapability({
      ...withLocks,
      hasAudio: true,
      canRender: async () =>
        result({canRender: false, issues: [videoIssue, audioIssue], resolvedAudioCodec: null}),
    });

    assert.equal(capability.kind, "unsupported");
    assert.match(capability.message, /Video codec "h264" cannot be encoded/u);
  });

  it("never accepts another audio codec than AAC (AINV-9)", async () => {
    const capability = await checkBrowserRenderCapability({
      ...withLocks,
      hasAudio: true,
      canRender: async () => result({resolvedAudioCodec: "opus"}),
    });

    assert.equal(capability.kind, "audio-unsupported");
  });

  it("decides only after the probe settles, so the WASM encoder registration it awaits is part of the decision", async () => {
    let finishRegistration = () => undefined;
    const registered = new Promise((resolve) => {
      finishRegistration = resolve;
    });
    let decided = false;
    const pending = checkBrowserRenderCapability({
      ...withLocks,
      hasAudio: true,
      canRender: async () => {
        await registered;

        return result();
      },
    }).then((capability) => {
      decided = true;

      return capability;
    });

    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 10));
    assert.equal(decided, false);
    finishRegistration();
    assert.deepEqual(await pending, {kind: "ready"});
  });

  it("a transaction with an unsupported audio capability does not take the render lock", async () => {
    let locked = false;
    const outcome = await startBrowserRenderTransaction(exampleStory, {
      signal: new AbortController().signal,
      checkCapability: async () => ({kind: "audio-unsupported", message: AUDIO_UNSUPPORTED_MESSAGE}),
      acquireLock: async () => {
        locked = true;

        return {mode: "owner", release: () => undefined};
      },
    });

    assert.deepEqual(outcome, {kind: "unsupported", message: AUDIO_UNSUPPORTED_MESSAGE});
    assert.equal(locked, false);
  });

  it("the transaction's own capability check asks for audio exactly when the Story has audio", async () => {
    const source = await readFile(new URL("../src/web/browserRender.ts", import.meta.url), "utf8");

    assert.match(source, /checkBrowserRenderCapability\(\{hasAudio: storyHasAudio\(story\)\}\)/u);
  });
});

describe("AUDIO-005 capability cache per storyHasAudio value", () => {
  it("probes each value at most once and never probes audio for a silent Story", async () => {
    const calls = [];
    const cache = createRenderCapabilityCache(async ({hasAudio}) => {
      calls.push(hasAudio);

      return hasAudio ? {kind: "audio-unsupported", message: "no aac"} : {kind: "ready"};
    });

    assert.equal(cache.started(false), false);
    assert.deepEqual(await cache.get(false), {kind: "ready"});
    assert.deepEqual(await cache.get(false), {kind: "ready"});
    assert.deepEqual(calls, [false]);
    assert.equal(cache.started(true), false);

    // The first Story with audio pays for the audio probe once; later ones reuse it.
    assert.deepEqual(await cache.get(true), {kind: "audio-unsupported", message: "no aac"});
    assert.deepEqual(await cache.get(true), {kind: "audio-unsupported", message: "no aac"});
    assert.deepEqual(await cache.get(false), {kind: "ready"});
    assert.deepEqual(calls, [false, true]);
  });

  it("shares one in-flight probe between concurrent callers", async () => {
    let probes = 0;
    const cache = createRenderCapabilityCache(async () => {
      probes += 1;

      return {kind: "ready"};
    });

    await Promise.all([cache.get(true), cache.get(true), cache.get(true)]);
    assert.equal(probes, 1);
  });
});
```

Create `src/web/renderCapabilityCache.ts`:

```ts
import type {BrowserRenderCapability} from "./browserRender.ts";

export type RenderCapabilityCache = {
  /**
   * The capability for a Story with (`true`) or without (`false`) audio. The
   * probe for each value runs at most once; the other value is never probed
   * until a Story needs it, so a silent Story never pays for the audio probe.
   */
  get(hasAudio: boolean): Promise<BrowserRenderCapability>;
  /** Whether the probe for this value was started. */
  started(hasAudio: boolean): boolean;
};

export const createRenderCapabilityCache = (
  check: (options: {hasAudio: boolean}) => Promise<BrowserRenderCapability>,
): RenderCapabilityCache => {
  const entries = new Map<boolean, Promise<BrowserRenderCapability>>();

  return {
    get(hasAudio) {
      let entry = entries.get(hasAudio);

      if (entry === undefined) {
        entry = check({hasAudio});
        entries.set(hasAudio, entry);
      }

      return entry;
    },
    started: (hasAudio) => entries.has(hasAudio),
  };
};
```

---

### Task 9: `AudioDecodeCache`

**Files:**
- Create: `src/web/assetLibrary/audioDecodeCache.ts`, `tests/audio-decode-cache.test.mjs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `isDecodeComplete`, `decodeAudioPeaks(blob, metadata, deps?)` (AUDIO-003, browser only), `AudioMetadata`.
- Produces: `AudioDecodeCheck = {ok: true} | {ok: false; detail: string}`; `AUDIO_DECODE_TIMEOUT_MS = 30_000`; `class AudioDecodeCache` with `constructor(decode?: (blob, metadata, deps: {signal: AbortSignal}) => Promise<{decodedSeconds: number}>, options?: {timeoutMs?, timers?})` (default: `decodeAudioPeaks`; every decode gets its own abort signal and a 30 s timeout, after which it is aborted and settles as `{ok: false, detail: "the decode did not finish within 30 s"}` so a stuck decode never blocks the queue), `verify(digest, blob, metadata, deps?: {signal?: AbortSignal}): Promise<AudioDecodeCheck>`, `has(digest): boolean`, `invalidate(digests: Iterable<string>): void`, `clear(): void`. Rules (the integrity cache's, ASSET-004 R14): one decode at a time page-wide, one in-flight decode per digest shared by its callers, an invalidation wins over an in-flight result (returned to its callers, not cached), aborting only stops one caller from waiting (a queued decode nobody waits for is never started), a failure is never cached, a decode error or a truncated decode is `{ok: false, detail}`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `tests/audio-decode-cache.test.mjs`:

```js
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import {describe, it} from "node:test";
import {
  AUDIO_DECODE_TIMEOUT_MS,
  AudioDecodeCache,
} from "../src/web/assetLibrary/audioDecodeCache.ts";

const metadata = (durationSeconds = 10) => ({
  container: "wave",
  codec: "pcm",
  durationSeconds,
  sampleRate: 48_000,
  channels: 2,
});
const blob = () => new Blob(["audio"]);
const digestOf = (n) => n.toString(16).padStart(64, "0");

/** A decode the test settles by hand; `calls` records every digest it was started for. */
const controlledDecode = () => {
  const calls = [];
  const pending = [];
  let active = 0;
  let maxActive = 0;
  const decode = (_blob, meta) => {
    calls.push(meta);
    active += 1;
    maxActive = Math.max(maxActive, active);

    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      pending.push({
        resolve: (decodedSeconds) => {
          active -= 1;
          resolve({decodedSeconds});
        },
        reject: (error) => {
          active -= 1;
          reject(error);
        },
      });
    });
  };

  return {decode, calls, pending, maxActive: () => maxActive};
};
const tick = () => new Promise((resolve) => setImmediate(resolve));

describe("AudioDecodeCache", () => {
  it("decodes a source once per session and answers later renders from the cache", async () => {
    const control = controlledDecode();
    const cache = new AudioDecodeCache(control.decode);
    const first = cache.verify(digestOf(1), blob(), metadata());

    await tick();
    control.pending[0].resolve(10);
    assert.deepEqual(await first, {ok: true});
    assert.equal(cache.has(digestOf(1)), true);
    assert.deepEqual(await cache.verify(digestOf(1), blob(), metadata()), {ok: true});
    assert.equal(control.calls.length, 1);
  });

  it("applies the D-9 truncation rule: 0.2 s short passes, 0.3 s short fails and is not cached", async () => {
    const outcomes = [];

    for (const decoded of [9.8, 9.7, 0]) {
      const cache = new AudioDecodeCache(async () => ({decodedSeconds: decoded}));

      outcomes.push([decoded, await cache.verify(digestOf(1), blob(), metadata()), cache.has(digestOf(1))]);
    }

    assert.deepEqual(outcomes[0], [9.8, {ok: true}, true]);
    assert.deepEqual(outcomes[1], [9.7, {ok: false, detail: "only 9.70 s of 10.00 s could be decoded"}, false]);
    assert.deepEqual(outcomes[2], [0, {ok: false, detail: "only 0.00 s of 10.00 s could be decoded"}, false]);
  });

  it("turns a decode error into a failure with the error's message, and decodes again next time", async () => {
    let calls = 0;
    const cache = new AudioDecodeCache(async () => {
      calls += 1;

      if (calls === 1) {
        throw new Error("This audio file could not be decoded.");
      }

      return {decodedSeconds: 10};
    });

    assert.deepEqual(await cache.verify(digestOf(1), blob(), metadata()), {
      ok: false,
      detail: "This audio file could not be decoded.",
    });
    assert.deepEqual(await cache.verify(digestOf(1), blob(), metadata()), {ok: true});
    assert.equal(calls, 2);
  });

  it("shares one in-flight decode between callers of the same digest", async () => {
    const control = controlledDecode();
    const cache = new AudioDecodeCache(control.decode);
    const first = cache.verify(digestOf(1), blob(), metadata());
    const second = cache.verify(digestOf(1), blob(), metadata());

    await tick();
    assert.equal(control.calls.length, 1);
    control.pending[0].resolve(10);
    assert.deepEqual(await Promise.all([first, second]), [{ok: true}, {ok: true}]);
  });

  it("decodes one source at a time, page-wide", async () => {
    const control = controlledDecode();
    const cache = new AudioDecodeCache(control.decode);
    const all = [1, 2, 3].map((n) => cache.verify(digestOf(n), blob(), metadata()));

    for (let n = 0; n < 3; n += 1) {
      await tick();
      assert.equal(control.calls.length, n + 1);
      control.pending[n].resolve(10);
    }

    assert.deepEqual(await Promise.all(all), [{ok: true}, {ok: true}, {ok: true}]);
    assert.equal(control.maxActive(), 1);
  });

  it("re-decodes after an invalidation of a cached digest", async () => {
    let calls = 0;
    const cache = new AudioDecodeCache(async () => {
      calls += 1;

      return {decodedSeconds: 10};
    });

    await cache.verify(digestOf(1), blob(), metadata());
    await cache.verify(digestOf(2), blob(), metadata());
    cache.invalidate([digestOf(1)]);
    assert.equal(cache.has(digestOf(1)), false);
    assert.equal(cache.has(digestOf(2)), true);
    await cache.verify(digestOf(1), blob(), metadata());
    assert.equal(calls, 3);
    cache.clear();
    assert.equal(cache.has(digestOf(2)), false);
  });

  it("an invalidation while a decode is in flight wins: the result reaches its callers but is not cached", async () => {
    const control = controlledDecode();
    const cache = new AudioDecodeCache(control.decode);
    const first = cache.verify(digestOf(1), blob(), metadata());

    await tick();
    cache.invalidate([digestOf(1)]);
    control.pending[0].resolve(10);
    assert.deepEqual(await first, {ok: true});
    assert.equal(cache.has(digestOf(1)), false);

    // The next caller starts a fresh decode.
    const second = cache.verify(digestOf(1), blob(), metadata());

    await tick();
    assert.equal(control.calls.length, 2);
    control.pending[1].resolve(10);
    assert.deepEqual(await second, {ok: true});
    assert.equal(cache.has(digestOf(1)), true);
  });

  it("aborting stops one caller from waiting without poisoning the cache or the other caller", async () => {
    const control = controlledDecode();
    const cache = new AudioDecodeCache(control.decode);
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const waiting = cache.verify(digestOf(1), blob(), metadata(), {signal: controller.signal});
    const other = cache.verify(digestOf(1), blob(), metadata());

    await tick();
    controller.abort();
    await assert.rejects(waiting, {name: "AbortError"});
    control.pending[0].resolve(10);
    assert.deepEqual(await other, {ok: true});
    assert.equal(cache.has(digestOf(1)), true);
    assert.equal(control.calls.length, 1);
  });

  it("a decode that nobody waits for any more is never started", async () => {
    const control = controlledDecode();
    const cache = new AudioDecodeCache(control.decode);
    const blocker = cache.verify(digestOf(1), blob(), metadata());
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const queued = cache.verify(digestOf(2), blob(), metadata(), {signal: controller.signal});

    await tick();
    controller.abort();
    await assert.rejects(queued, {name: "AbortError"});
    control.pending[0].resolve(10);
    await blocker;
    await tick();
    assert.equal(control.calls.length, 1);

    // A later caller of the cancelled digest decodes it normally.
    const later = cache.verify(digestOf(2), blob(), metadata());

    await tick();
    assert.equal(control.calls.length, 2);
    control.pending[1].resolve(10);
    assert.deepEqual(await later, {ok: true});
  });

  it("a decode that never settles fails after 30 s, is aborted, and does not block the next decode", async () => {
    const timers = [];
    const cleared = [];
    const signals = [];
    let calls = 0;
    const cache = new AudioDecodeCache(
      (_blob, _meta, deps) => {
        calls += 1;
        signals.push(deps.signal);

        // The first decode never settles; later ones answer at once.
        return calls === 1
          ? new Promise(() => undefined)
          : Promise.resolve({decodedSeconds: 10});
      },
      {
        timers: {
          setTimeout: (callback, milliseconds) => {
            timers.push({callback, milliseconds});

            return timers.length;
          },
          clearTimeout: (handle) => cleared.push(handle),
        },
      },
    );

    assert.equal(AUDIO_DECODE_TIMEOUT_MS, 30_000);

    const stuck = cache.verify(digestOf(1), blob(), metadata());
    const next = cache.verify(digestOf(2), blob(), metadata());

    await tick();
    assert.equal(timers.length, 1);
    assert.equal(timers[0].milliseconds, 30_000);
    assert.equal(signals[0].aborted, false);

    timers[0].callback();
    assert.deepEqual(await stuck, {
      ok: false,
      detail: "the decode did not finish within 30 s",
    });
    assert.equal(signals[0].aborted, true);
    assert.equal(cache.has(digestOf(1)), false);

    // The queue moved on: the second source decodes, and its timer is cleared.
    assert.deepEqual(await next, {ok: true});
    assert.deepEqual(cleared, [2]);
  });

  it("an already-aborted signal rejects before any decode", async () => {
    const control = controlledDecode();
    const cache = new AudioDecodeCache(control.decode);
    const controller = new AbortController();

    controller.abort();
    await assert.rejects(
      cache.verify(digestOf(1), blob(), metadata(), {signal: controller.signal}),
      {name: "AbortError"},
    );
    assert.equal(control.calls.length, 0);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

Create `src/web/assetLibrary/audioDecodeCache.ts`:

```ts
import {isDecodeComplete} from "../../audio/decodeCheck.ts";
import type {AudioMetadata} from "../../localAssets/audioInspection.ts";
import {decodeAudioPeaks} from "./audioPeaks.ts";

/** The outcome of one session decode of a local audio payload (AINV-8). */
export type AudioDecodeCheck = {ok: true} | {ok: false; detail: string};

/** `decodeAudioPeaks` reduced to what the check needs; tests inject a fake. */
export type DecodeAudio = (
  blob: Blob,
  metadata: AudioMetadata,
  deps: {signal: AbortSignal},
) => Promise<{decodedSeconds: number}>;

/**
 * One decode runs at a time page-wide, so a decode that never settles would
 * block every later render: it fails after this long instead (a 300 s MP3
 * decodes in about a second).
 */
export const AUDIO_DECODE_TIMEOUT_MS = 30_000;

export type DecodeTimers = {
  setTimeout(callback: () => void, milliseconds: number): unknown;
  clearTimeout(handle: unknown): void;
};

/** A decode that is queued or running, shared by every caller of a digest. */
type InFlight = {
  promise: Promise<AudioDecodeCheck>;
  waiters: number;
  started: boolean;
  cancelled: boolean;
};

const secondsText = (seconds: number): string => seconds.toFixed(2);

/**
 * Per-page-session cache of full decodes of local audio payloads. The import
 * decode does not count: after a reload, bytes whose metadata still parses
 * could fail to decode, and only a decode proves the renderer will not stall
 * on them. A Story's first render therefore pays at most one decode per
 * distinct source (~1 s for a 300 s MP3), later renders none. A decode that
 * does not settle within `AUDIO_DECODE_TIMEOUT_MS` is aborted and is a failure.
 *
 * It follows the integrity cache's rules (ASSET-004 R14, D-8): one decode at a
 * time page-wide, one in-flight decode per digest shared by its callers, an
 * invalidation (a library change) wins over an in-flight result, and aborting
 * only stops one caller from waiting. A failure is never cached.
 */
export class AudioDecodeCache {
  private readonly passed = new Set<string>();
  // Dropped by `invalidate`/`clear`, so an entry that is no longer here is stale:
  // its result is not cached and later callers start a fresh decode.
  private readonly inFlight = new Map<string, InFlight>();
  private queueTail: Promise<void> = Promise.resolve();
  private readonly decode: DecodeAudio;
  private readonly timeoutMs: number;
  private readonly timers: DecodeTimers;

  constructor(
    decode: DecodeAudio = (blob, metadata, deps) =>
      decodeAudioPeaks(blob, metadata, deps),
    options: {timeoutMs?: number; timers?: DecodeTimers} = {},
  ) {
    this.decode = decode;
    this.timeoutMs = options.timeoutMs ?? AUDIO_DECODE_TIMEOUT_MS;
    this.timers = options.timers ?? {
      setTimeout: (callback, milliseconds) => setTimeout(callback, milliseconds),
      clearTimeout: (handle) => clearTimeout(handle as ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>),
    };
  }

  /** One decode with its own abort signal and timeout. */
  private decodeWithTimeout(
    blob: Blob,
    metadata: AudioMetadata,
  ): Promise<{decodedSeconds: number}> {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const controller = new AbortController();
      const timer = this.timers.setTimeout(() => {
        controller.abort();
        reject(
          new Error(
            `the decode did not finish within ${this.timeoutMs / 1000} s`,
          ),
        );
      }, this.timeoutMs);

      this.decode(blob, metadata, {signal: controller.signal}).then(
        (result) => {
          this.timers.clearTimeout(timer);
          resolve(result);
        },
        (error: unknown) => {
          this.timers.clearTimeout(timer);
          reject(error);
        },
      );
    });
  }

  /** True when this digest decoded completely earlier in the session. */
  has(digest: string): boolean {
    return this.passed.has(digest);
  }

  invalidate(digests: Iterable<string>): void {
    for (const digest of digests) {
      this.passed.delete(digest);
      this.inFlight.delete(digest);
    }
  }

  clear(): void {
    this.passed.clear();
    this.inFlight.clear();
  }

  /**
   * Decodes `blob` completely (D-9), joining a decode already queued or
   * running for `digest`. Aborting `signal` only stops this caller from
   * waiting: the shared decode keeps going for the others, and is skipped when
   * it has not started and nobody waits for it any more.
   */
  async verify(
    digest: string,
    blob: Blob,
    metadata: AudioMetadata,
    deps: {signal?: AbortSignal} = {},
  ): Promise<AudioDecodeCheck> {
    const {signal} = deps;

    signal?.throwIfAborted();

    if (this.passed.has(digest)) {
      return {ok: true};
    }

    let entry = this.inFlight.get(digest);

    if (entry === undefined) {
      entry = this.startDecode(digest, blob, metadata);
    }

    const joined = entry;

    joined.waiters += 1;

    let left = false;
    // Synchronous, so the abort handler runs it before the queued task (a
    // microtask) can start: a decode nobody waits for any more is never begun.
    const leave = () => {
      if (left) {
        return;
      }

      left = true;
      joined.waiters -= 1;

      if (joined.waiters === 0 && !joined.started) {
        joined.cancelled = true;

        if (this.inFlight.get(digest) === joined) {
          this.inFlight.delete(digest);
        }
      }
    };

    if (signal === undefined) {
      try {
        return await joined.promise;
      } finally {
        leave();
      }
    }

    try {
      return await new Promise<AudioDecodeCheck>((resolve, reject) => {
        const onAbort = () => {
          leave();
          reject(signal.reason);
        };

        signal.addEventListener("abort", onAbort, {once: true});
        joined.promise.then(
          (result) => {
            signal.removeEventListener("abort", onAbort);
            resolve(result);
          },
          (error) => {
            signal.removeEventListener("abort", onAbort);
            reject(error);
          },
        );
      });
    } finally {
      leave();
    }
  }

  private startDecode(
    digest: string,
    blob: Blob,
    metadata: AudioMetadata,
  ): InFlight {
    const entry: InFlight = {
      promise: undefined as unknown as Promise<AudioDecodeCheck>,
      waiters: 0,
      started: false,
      cancelled: false,
    };
    const task = async (): Promise<AudioDecodeCheck> => {
      if (entry.cancelled) {
        return {ok: false, detail: "the decode was cancelled"};
      }

      entry.started = true;

      try {
        let result: AudioDecodeCheck;

        try {
          const decoded = await this.decodeWithTimeout(blob, metadata);

          result = isDecodeComplete(decoded.decodedSeconds, metadata.durationSeconds)
            ? {ok: true}
            : {
                ok: false,
                detail: `only ${secondsText(decoded.decodedSeconds)} s of ${secondsText(metadata.durationSeconds)} s could be decoded`,
              };
        } catch (error) {
          result = {
            ok: false,
            detail: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
          };
        }

        // Invalidation wins: a stale decode is returned to the callers that
        // joined it but never cached.
        if (result.ok && this.inFlight.get(digest) === entry) {
          this.passed.add(digest);
        }

        return result;
      } finally {
        if (this.inFlight.get(digest) === entry) {
          this.inFlight.delete(digest);
        }
      }
    };

    // One decode at a time, page-wide.
    const run = this.queueTail.then(task);

    this.queueTail = run.then(
      () => undefined,
      () => undefined,
    );
    entry.promise = run;
    this.inFlight.set(digest, entry);

    return entry;
  }
}
```
