### Task 10: The audio render preparation (frozen sources, re-check, preflight)

**Files:**
- Create: `src/web/audioRenderPreparation.ts`, `tests/audio-render-preparation.test.mjs`, `tests/audio-duration-parity.test.mjs` (one fixture Story gives the same `audioDurations` and the same frame total to browser readiness and to the CLI props, AINV-3)
- Modify: `src/audio/readiness.ts` (`compileFrozenAudioPlan`), `scripts/audioRender.ts` (use it), `src/web/localAssetState.ts` (the preparation type), `tests/story-audio-durations.test.mjs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 9 (`AudioDecodeCache`), `createLocalAssetRenderPreparation` (ASSET-004: verifies and leases the local audio payloads exactly like images), `buildAudioSourceMap`, `inspectAudio`, `storyAudioDurations`, `ResolvedStoryLocalAssetState`, `AssetLibraryStore.getAssetRow`.
- Produces:
  - `src/audio/readiness.ts`: `compileFrozenAudioPlan(story, audioDurations, audioSources): AudioPlan` (the plan of a frozen map with `"explicit"` semantics; both renderers and the CLI use it).
  - `src/web/localAssetState.ts`: the `ok` variant of `LocalAssetRenderPreparation` gains optional `audioSources?: AudioSourceMap` and `localAudioDurations?: AudioDurationMap`.
  - `src/web/audioRenderPreparation.ts`: `AUDIO_CHANGED_MESSAGE = "Audio changed while preparing the render. Try again."`; `BundledRenderState = {readonly kind: string; readonly blob?: Blob}`; `createAudioRenderPreparation(input: {story; resolved: ResolvedStoryLocalAssetState | null; prepareLocal: ((signal) => Promise<LocalAssetRenderPreparation>) | null; getBundledStates: () => ReadonlyMap<BundledAudio, BundledRenderState>; store: AssetLibraryStore | null; decodeCache: AudioDecodeCache; onStatus?: (message: string) => void; deps?: {createObjectUrl?; revokeObjectUrl?}}): (signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<LocalAssetRenderPreparation>`; `CHECKING_AUDIO_MESSAGE = "Checking audio…"` is passed to `onStatus` once, before the first inspection or decode. It runs `prepareLocal`, requires every referenced bundled id to be `ready` with a `blob` (else `{ok: false, message: AUDIO_CHANGED_MESSAGE}`), freezes `buildAudioSourceMap(story, preparedLocalSources, bundled render-owned object URLs)`, re-checks the frozen map with `compileFrozenAudioPlan` (any `pending-audio` or `missing-audio` is `{ok: false, message: AUDIO_CHANGED_MESSAGE}`; `story-too-long-for-audio` is `{ok: false}` with `This Story is longer than 300 s with its voices (N s).`), and preflights: bundled blobs by `inspectAudio` within 1 ms of the catalog duration (their decode is the store's), local payloads by `decodeCache.verify` (a decode in this page session). A preflight failure rejects with `Audio file <label> cannot be used for rendering.`; everything acquired (object URLs, the local lease) is released on every path that does not return `{ok: true}`; the success result's `release()` revokes the bundled URLs and releases the local lease, once.
- Edits to existing tests: `tests/story-audio-durations.test.mjs` lists the modules allowed to call `storyAudioDurations(` in `src/web` (it now includes `src/web/audioRenderPreparation.ts`, which needs the render's own durations).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `tests/audio-render-preparation.test.mjs`:

```js
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import {readFile} from "node:fs/promises";
import {describe, it} from "node:test";
import {inspectAudio, audioMimeTypeFor} from "../src/localAssets/audioInspection.ts";
import {sha256Hex} from "../src/localAssets/hash.ts";
import {buildLocalAssetRef} from "../src/localAssets/refs.ts";
import {AudioDecodeCache} from "../src/web/assetLibrary/audioDecodeCache.ts";
import {IntegrityCache} from "../src/web/assetLibrary/integrity.ts";
import {
  AUDIO_CHANGED_MESSAGE,
  CHECKING_AUDIO_MESSAGE,
  createAudioRenderPreparation,
} from "../src/web/audioRenderPreparation.ts";
import {startBrowserRenderTransaction} from "../src/web/browserRender.ts";
import {
  createLocalAssetRenderPreparation,
  resolveStoryLocalAssets,
} from "../src/web/localAssetState.ts";
import {buildWav, fixtureBytes} from "./helpers/audioBytes.mjs";
import {
  createMemoryAssetStore,
  createMemoryLockManager,
} from "./helpers/memoryAssetStore.mjs";

const scene = (overrides = {}) => ({
  type: "dialogue",
  pose: "formal",
  background: "office",
  text: "Hi",
  duration: 3,
  ...overrides,
});
const dingStory = {title: "Ding", scenes: [scene({sfx: {src: "ding"}})]};
const row = (label) => ({label, originalFilename: `${label}.wav`, createdAt: "2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z"});
const signal = () => new AbortController().signal;
const tick = () => new Promise((resolve) => setImmediate(resolve));

/** A fake object-URL API that records what the render created and revoked. */
const fakeUrls = () => {
  const created = [];
  const revoked = [];

  return {
    created,
    revoked,
    deps: {
      createObjectUrl: (blob) => {
        created.push(blob);

        return `blob:render/${created.length}`;
      },
      revokeObjectUrl: (url) => revoked.push(url),
    },
  };
};

/** The real bundled ding.wav as the store would hold it once `ready`. */
const readyDing = async () => ({
  kind: "ready",
  blob: new Blob([await readFile(new URL("../public/audio/sfx/ding.wav", import.meta.url))]),
});

/** A fake pool + fake local preparation for a Story with no verified payloads. */
const fakeLocal = (sources = {}) => {
  const events = [];

  return {
    events,
    prepare: async () => ({
      ok: true,
      localAssetSources: Object.freeze({...sources}),
      release: () => events.push("local-release"),
    }),
  };
};

const prepareFor = (story, options = {}) => {
  const urls = options.urls ?? fakeUrls();
  const decodeCache = options.decodeCache ?? new AudioDecodeCache(async () => ({decodedSeconds: 2.2}));

  return {
    urls,
    decodeCache,
    run: createAudioRenderPreparation({
      story,
      resolved: options.resolved ?? null,
      prepareLocal: options.prepareLocal ?? null,
      getBundledStates: () => new Map(Object.entries(options.bundled ?? {})),
      store: options.store ?? null,
      decodeCache,
      onStatus: options.onStatus,
      deps: urls.deps,
    }),
  };
};

describe("bundled audio in the render preparation", () => {
  it("freezes a render-owned object URL of the store's blob under the bundled id and revokes it with the lease", async () => {
    const ding = await readyDing();
    const local = fakeLocal();
    const {urls, run} = prepareFor(dingStory, {bundled: {ding}, prepareLocal: local.prepare});
    const result = await run(signal());

    assert.equal(result.ok, true);
    assert.deepEqual(result.audioSources, {ding: {kind: "url", url: "blob:render/1"}});
    assert.equal(Object.isFrozen(result.audioSources), true);
    assert.equal(urls.created[0], ding.blob);
    assert.deepEqual(result.localAudioDurations, {});

    result.release();
    result.release();
    assert.deepEqual(urls.revoked, ["blob:render/1"]);
    assert.deepEqual(local.events, ["local-release"]);
  });

  it("does not decode the bundled blob again: the store's ready state is the decode", async () => {
    let decodes = 0;
    const decodeCache = new AudioDecodeCache(async () => {
      decodes += 1;

      return {decodedSeconds: 1.2};
    });
    const {run} = prepareFor(dingStory, {bundled: {ding: await readyDing()}, decodeCache});

    assert.equal((await run(signal())).ok, true);
    assert.equal(decodes, 0);
  });

  for (const [title, state] of [
    ["absent", undefined],
    ["loading", {kind: "loading"}],
    ["retrying", {kind: "retrying", attempt: 1}],
    ["fetch-failed", {kind: "fetch-failed", message: "Network"}],
    ["unusable", {kind: "unusable", message: "bad bytes"}],
    ["ready without a blob", {kind: "ready"}],
  ]) {
    it(`returns assets-changed without creating a URL when the bundled id is ${title} at the click, releasing the local lease`, async () => {
      const local = fakeLocal();
      const {urls, run} = prepareFor(dingStory, {
        bundled: state === undefined ? {} : {ding: state},
        prepareLocal: local.prepare,
      });
      const result = await run(signal());

      assert.deepEqual(result, {ok: false, message: AUDIO_CHANGED_MESSAGE});
      assert.equal(AUDIO_CHANGED_MESSAGE, "Audio changed while preparing the render. Try again.");
      assert.equal(urls.created.length, 0);
      assert.deepEqual(local.events, ["local-release"]);
    });
  }

  it("rejects a bundled blob that is not the catalog length, naming the sound by its label", async () => {
    const local = fakeLocal();
    const {urls, run} = prepareFor(dingStory, {
      bundled: {ding: {kind: "ready", blob: new Blob([buildWav({seconds: 5})])}},
      prepareLocal: local.prepare,
    });

    await assert.rejects(run(signal()), /^Error: Audio file Ding cannot be used for rendering\.$/u);
    assert.deepEqual(urls.revoked, ["blob:render/1"]);
    assert.deepEqual(local.events, ["local-release"]);
  });

  it("rejects a bundled blob that is a video file", async () => {
    const {run} = prepareFor(dingStory, {
      bundled: {ding: {kind: "ready", blob: new Blob([fixtureBytes("with-video.mp4")])}},
    });

    await assert.rejects(run(signal()), /Audio file Ding cannot be used for rendering\./u);
  });

  it("the frozen-map re-check refuses a source map that lost a local source, before any preflight", async () => {
    let decodes = 0;
    const voice = `local:audio:sha256:${"b".repeat(64)}`;
    const story = {title: "Voice", scenes: [scene({voice: {src: voice}})]};
    const local = fakeLocal({});
    const decodeCache = new AudioDecodeCache(async () => {
      decodes += 1;

      return {decodedSeconds: 2.2};
    });
    const {run} = prepareFor(story, {prepareLocal: local.prepare, decodeCache});
    const result = await run(signal());

    assert.deepEqual(result, {ok: false, message: AUDIO_CHANGED_MESSAGE});
    assert.equal(decodes, 0);
    assert.deepEqual(local.events, ["local-release"]);
  });

  it("says what the wait is, once, before the preflight and not when the sources changed", async () => {
    const statuses = [];
    const ready = prepareFor(dingStory, {
      bundled: {ding: await readyDing()},
      onStatus: (message) => statuses.push(message),
    });

    assert.equal((await ready.run(signal())).ok, true);
    assert.deepEqual(statuses, ["Checking audio…"]);
    assert.equal(CHECKING_AUDIO_MESSAGE, "Checking audio…");

    const changed = prepareFor(dingStory, {
      bundled: {},
      onStatus: (message) => statuses.push(message),
    });

    assert.equal((await changed.run(signal())).ok, false);
    assert.deepEqual(statuses, ["Checking audio…"]);
  });

  it("a Story that is longer than the browser can render with its audio gets the length sentence, not the 'changed' one", async () => {
    const long = {title: "Long", scenes: [scene({duration: 3601, sfx: {src: "ding"}})]};
    const local = fakeLocal();
    const {urls, run} = prepareFor(long, {bundled: {ding: await readyDing()}, prepareLocal: local.prepare});

    assert.deepEqual(await run(signal()), {
      ok: false,
      message: "This Story is longer than 300 s with its voices (3601 s).",
    });
    assert.deepEqual(urls.revoked, ["blob:render/1"]);
    assert.deepEqual(local.events, ["local-release"]);
  });

  it("returns the local preparation's own failure unchanged", async () => {
    const failure = {ok: false, message: "Local assets changed while preparing the render. Try again."};
    const {run} = prepareFor(dingStory, {
      bundled: {ding: await readyDing()},
      prepareLocal: async () => failure,
    });

    assert.deepEqual(await run(signal()), failure);
  });
});

describe("local audio in the render preparation", () => {
  const seedVoice = async (store, name = "tone-440-2.2s.wav", label = "Voice take") => {
    const bytes = fixtureBytes(name);
    const digest = await sha256Hex(bytes);
    const inspected = await inspectAudio(bytes);

    assert.equal(inspected.ok, true);

    const {container, codec, durationSeconds, sampleRate, channels} = inspected.metadata;
    const ref = buildLocalAssetRef("audio", digest);

    store.setRaw("assets", ref, row(label));
    store.setRaw("payloadMeta", digest, {
      kind: "audio",
      mimeType: audioMimeTypeFor(container),
      byteSize: bytes.length,
      container,
      codec,
      durationSeconds,
      sampleRate,
      channels,
    });
    store.setRaw("blobs", digest, {blob: new Blob([bytes])});

    return {ref, digest, durationSeconds};
  };
  const setup = async (name, label) => {
    const store = createMemoryAssetStore();
    const voice = await seedVoice(store, name, label);
    const story = {
      title: "Voice and ding",
      scenes: [scene({voice: {src: voice.ref}, sfx: {src: "ding"}, duration: "auto"})],
    };
    const resolved = await resolveStoryLocalAssets(
      story,
      {kind: "ready", store},
      new IntegrityCache(),
    );
    const pool = {acquired: [], released: []};

    assert.equal(resolved.allReady, true);

    return {
      store,
      voice,
      story,
      resolved,
      pool,
      prepareLocal: createLocalAssetRenderPreparation({
        resolved,
        store,
        locks: createMemoryLockManager(),
        pool: {
          acquire: (digest) => {
            pool.acquired.push(digest);

            return `blob:lease/${digest.slice(0, 4)}`;
          },
          release: (digest) => pool.released.push(digest),
        },
      }),
    };
  };

  it("freezes lease URLs for the voice and render-owned URLs for the bundled sfx, with the verified duration", async () => {
    const {story, voice, resolved, store, prepareLocal, pool} = await setup();
    const prepared = prepareFor(story, {
      resolved,
      store,
      prepareLocal,
      bundled: {ding: await readyDing()},
    });
    const result = await prepared.run(signal());

    assert.equal(result.ok, true);
    assert.deepEqual(result.audioSources, {
      [voice.ref]: {kind: "url", url: `blob:lease/${voice.digest.slice(0, 4)}`},
      ding: {kind: "url", url: "blob:render/1"},
    });
    assert.deepEqual(result.localAudioDurations, {[voice.ref]: 2.2});
    assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(result.localAssetSources), [voice.ref]);

    result.release();
    assert.deepEqual(pool.released, [voice.digest]);
    assert.deepEqual(prepared.urls.revoked, ["blob:render/1"]);
  });

  it("decodes each local source once per session: a second render of the same Story does not decode", async () => {
    const {story, resolved, store, prepareLocal} = await setup();
    const decodeCache = new AudioDecodeCache(
      (() => {
        let calls = 0;

        return async () => {
          calls += 1;
          assert.equal(calls, 1, "decoded twice");

          return {decodedSeconds: 2.2};
        };
      })(),
    );

    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 2; attempt += 1) {
      const result = await prepareFor(story, {
        resolved,
        store,
        prepareLocal,
        decodeCache,
        bundled: {ding: await readyDing()},
      }).run(signal());

      assert.equal(result.ok, true);
      result.release();
    }
  });

  it("rejects a voice whose session decode fails, naming it by its library label, and releases everything", async () => {
    const {story, resolved, store, prepareLocal, pool} = await setup();
    const decodeCache = new AudioDecodeCache(async () => {
      throw new Error("This audio file could not be decoded.");
    });
    const prepared = prepareFor(story, {
      resolved,
      store,
      prepareLocal,
      decodeCache,
      bundled: {ding: await readyDing()},
    });

    await assert.rejects(prepared.run(signal()), /^Error: Audio file Voice take cannot be used for rendering\.$/u);
    assert.equal(pool.released.length, 1);
    assert.deepEqual(prepared.urls.revoked, ["blob:render/1"]);
  });

  it("rejects a truncated decode (0.3 s short) and accepts one 0.2 s short", async () => {
    for (const [decoded, accepted] of [[1.9, false], [2.0, true]]) {
      const {story, resolved, store, prepareLocal} = await setup();
      const decodeCache = new AudioDecodeCache(async () => ({decodedSeconds: decoded}));
      const run = prepareFor(story, {
        resolved,
        store,
        prepareLocal,
        decodeCache,
        bundled: {ding: await readyDing()},
      }).run(signal());

      if (accepted) {
        assert.equal((await run).ok, true);
      } else {
        await assert.rejects(run, /Audio file Voice take cannot be used for rendering\./u);
      }
    }
  });

  it("aborting during the decode rejects with the abort and releases everything", async () => {
    const {story, resolved, store, prepareLocal, pool} = await setup();
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const decodeCache = new AudioDecodeCache(() => new Promise(() => undefined));
    const prepared = prepareFor(story, {
      resolved,
      store,
      prepareLocal,
      decodeCache,
      bundled: {ding: await readyDing()},
    });
    const running = prepared.run(controller.signal);

    await tick();
    await tick();
    controller.abort();
    await assert.rejects(running, {name: "AbortError"});
    assert.equal(pool.released.length, 1);
    assert.deepEqual(prepared.urls.revoked, ["blob:render/1"]);
  });
});

describe("a failed audio preflight inside the render transaction", () => {
  it("fails the render before any OPFS cleanup and releases the render lock", async () => {
    const calls = [];
    const decodeCache = new AudioDecodeCache(async () => ({decodedSeconds: 0}));
    const store = createMemoryAssetStore();
    const bytes = fixtureBytes("tone-440-2.2s.wav");
    const digest = await sha256Hex(bytes);
    const ref = buildLocalAssetRef("audio", digest);
    const inspected = await inspectAudio(bytes);
    const story = {title: "Voice", scenes: [scene({voice: {src: ref}})]};

    store.setRaw("assets", ref, row("Voice take"));
    store.setRaw("payloadMeta", digest, {
      kind: "audio",
      mimeType: "audio/wav",
      byteSize: bytes.length,
      container: inspected.metadata.container,
      codec: inspected.metadata.codec,
      durationSeconds: inspected.metadata.durationSeconds,
      sampleRate: inspected.metadata.sampleRate,
      channels: inspected.metadata.channels,
    });
    store.setRaw("blobs", digest, {blob: new Blob([bytes])});

    const resolved = await resolveStoryLocalAssets(
      story,
      {kind: "ready", store},
      new IntegrityCache(),
    );
    const outcome = await startBrowserRenderTransaction(story, {
      signal: signal(),
      checkCapability: async () => ({kind: "ready"}),
      acquireLock: async () => ({mode: "owner", release: () => calls.push("lock-release")}),
      cleanup: async () => {
        calls.push("cleanup");

        return {ok: true, attempts: 1};
      },
      renderStory: async () => {
        calls.push("render");
      },
      prepareLocalAssets: createAudioRenderPreparation({
        story,
        resolved,
        prepareLocal: createLocalAssetRenderPreparation({
          resolved,
          store,
          locks: createMemoryLockManager(),
          pool: {acquire: () => "blob:lease/x", release: () => calls.push("lease-release")},
        }),
        getBundledStates: () => new Map(),
        store,
        decodeCache,
      }),
    });

    assert.equal(outcome.kind, "failure");
    assert.match(outcome.error.message, /^Audio file Voice take cannot be used for rendering\.$/u);
    assert.deepEqual(calls, ["lease-release", "lock-release"]);
  });
});
```

```js
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import {mkdir, mkdtemp, rm, writeFile} from "node:fs/promises";
import {tmpdir} from "node:os";
import {join} from "node:path";
import {after, describe, it} from "node:test";
import {prepareCliAudio} from "../scripts/audioRender.ts";
import {stageLocalAssetsForStory} from "../scripts/localAssets.ts";
import {storyAudioDurations} from "../src/audio/storyAudio.ts";
import {audioMimeTypeFor, inspectAudio} from "../src/localAssets/audioInspection.ts";
import {sha256Hex} from "../src/localAssets/hash.ts";
import {buildLocalAssetRef} from "../src/localAssets/refs.ts";
import {compileTimeline} from "../src/story/timeline.ts";
import {IntegrityCache} from "../src/web/assetLibrary/integrity.ts";
import {evaluateAudioRenderReadiness} from "../src/web/audioRenderReadiness.ts";
import {resolveStoryLocalAssets} from "../src/web/localAssetState.ts";
import {fixtureBytes} from "./helpers/audioBytes.mjs";
import {createMemoryAssetStore} from "./helpers/memoryAssetStore.mjs";

// AINV-3: one duration per ref everywhere. One fixture Story and one set of
// inspected durations feed the browser readiness and the CLI props, and the two
// must agree (so `duration: auto` has the same length in both renders).

const directories = [];

after(async () => {
  for (const directory of directories) {
    await rm(directory, {recursive: true, force: true});
  }
});

describe("browser readiness and the CLI props use the same durations", () => {
  it("a Story with an imported voice (auto length) and a bundled sfx gets identical audioDurations and the same total frames", async () => {
    const bytes = fixtureBytes("tone-440-2.2s.wav");
    const digest = await sha256Hex(bytes);
    const ref = buildLocalAssetRef("audio", digest);
    const inspected = await inspectAudio(bytes);

    assert.equal(inspected.ok, true);

    const story = {
      title: "Parity",
      scenes: [
        {
          type: "intro",
          pose: "formal",
          background: "office",
          text: "One",
          sfx: {src: "ding"},
          voice: {src: ref},
          duration: "auto",
        },
      ],
    };

    // The browser: the library holds the file; the page resolves, verifies and reads its duration.
    const store = createMemoryAssetStore();
    const {container, codec, durationSeconds, sampleRate, channels} = inspected.metadata;

    store.setRaw("assets", ref, {
      label: "Voice",
      originalFilename: "voice.wav",
      createdAt: "2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z",
    });
    store.setRaw("payloadMeta", digest, {
      kind: "audio",
      mimeType: audioMimeTypeFor(container),
      byteSize: bytes.length,
      container,
      codec,
      durationSeconds,
      sampleRate,
      channels,
    });
    store.setRaw("blobs", digest, {blob: new Blob([bytes])});

    const localState = await resolveStoryLocalAssets(story, {kind: "ready", store}, new IntegrityCache());
    const pageMap = storyAudioDurations(story, {[ref]: inspected.metadata.durationSeconds});
    const readiness = evaluateAudioRenderReadiness({
      story,
      audioDurations: pageMap,
      localState,
      bundledStates: new Map([["ding", {kind: "ready"}]]),
    });

    assert.equal(readiness.block, null);

    // The CLI: the same file in local-assets/audio/, measured with the same inspectAudio.
    const root = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "tora-parity-root-"));
    const publicDir = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "tora-parity-public-"));

    directories.push(root, publicDir);
    await mkdir(join(root, "audio"));
    await writeFile(join(root, "audio", "any-name.wav"), bytes);

    const staged = await stageLocalAssetsForStory(story, {root, publicDir});
    const cli = await prepareCliAudio(story, {publicDir, localSources: staged.sources});

    assert.deepEqual(cli.audioDurations, {ding: 1.2, [ref]: 2.2});
    assert.deepEqual(readiness.audioDurations, cli.audioDurations);
    assert.deepEqual(pageMap, cli.audioDurations);
    assert.equal(
      compileTimeline(story, 30, readiness.audioDurations).durationInFrames,
      compileTimeline(story, 30, cli.audioDurations).durationInFrames,
    );
    // ceil((2.2 + 0.5) x 30) frames.
    assert.equal(compileTimeline(story, 30, cli.audioDurations).durationInFrames, 81);
  });
});
```

```ts
/**
 * The plan of a render's frozen `audioSources` (both renderers, AINV-5): bundled
 * ids resolve only through the map (`"explicit"`), so a source that is not in it
 * is pending, never a silent fallback. A render may start only when this plan has

export const compileFrozenAudioPlan = (
```

Create `src/web/audioRenderPreparation.ts`:

```ts
// The audio half of the browser render transaction's preparation (AUDIO-005):
// freezes the render's audio sources, re-checks the frozen map and preflights
// every audio source before OPFS is touched (AINV-8).
//
// It wraps the local-asset preparation (`createLocalAssetRenderPreparation`),
// which already verified, locked and leased the local audio payloads exactly
// like the images, and adds what only the audio needs: render-owned object URLs
// for the bundled sounds, the exact durations, and the preflight.

import {
  bundledAudioCatalog,
  isBundledAudio,
  type AudioDurationMap,
  type AudioRef,
  type BundledAudio,
} from "../audio/catalog.ts";
import {
  audioFileUnusableMessage,
  compileFrozenAudioPlan,
  storyTooLongWithVoicesMessage,
} from "../audio/readiness.ts";
import {buildAudioSourceMap, type AudioSource} from "../audio/sources.ts";
import {storyAudioDurations, storyAudioRefs} from "../audio/storyAudio.ts";
import {inspectAudio} from "../localAssets/audioInspection.ts";
import {isAudioPayload} from "../localAssets/readiness.ts";
import {isLocalAudioRef, type LocalAudioRef} from "../localAssets/refs.ts";
import {shortAssetId} from "../localAssets/sources.ts";
import {getStoryMetadata} from "../story/metadata.ts";
import type {Story} from "../story/types.ts";
import {VIDEO_FPS} from "../videoConfig.ts";
import type {AudioDecodeCache} from "./assetLibrary/audioDecodeCache.ts";
import type {AssetLibraryStore} from "./assetLibrary/store.ts";
import type {
  LocalAssetRenderPreparation,
  ResolvedStoryLocalAssetState,
} from "./localAssetState.ts";

/** Bundled durations are catalog constants; the blob must agree within this much (D-8). */
const BUNDLED_DURATION_TOLERANCE_SECONDS = 0.001;

export const AUDIO_CHANGED_MESSAGE =
  "Audio changed while preparing the render. Try again.";

/** Shown while the sources are inspected and decoded (up to one decode per distinct local file). */
export const CHECKING_AUDIO_MESSAGE = "Checking audio…";

/**
 * One entry of the bundled-audio store's snapshot as the render needs it
 * (AUDIO-004's `getBundledAudioStates()` values satisfy it): only a `ready`
 * entry carries the `blob` that the store inspected, fully decoded and
 * truncation-checked in this page session.
 */
export type BundledRenderState = {
  readonly kind: string;
  readonly blob?: Blob;
};

export type AudioRenderPreparationInput = {
  story: Story;
  /** The state the click verified; null when the Story has no local refs at all. */
  resolved: ResolvedStoryLocalAssetState | null;
  /** `createLocalAssetRenderPreparation(...)` for that state, or null when there is nothing local. */
  prepareLocal: ((signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<LocalAssetRenderPreparation>) | null;
  /** The store's snapshot at the moment of the call (frozen into the render here). */
  getBundledStates: () => ReadonlyMap<BundledAudio, BundledRenderState>;
  /** Names a local file in the preflight error; read only when a preflight fails. */
  store: AssetLibraryStore | null;
  decodeCache: AudioDecodeCache;
  /** Told once, before the first inspection or decode, so the page can say what the wait is. */
  onStatus?: (message: string) => void;
  deps?: {
    createObjectUrl?: (blob: Blob) => string;
    revokeObjectUrl?: (url: string) => void;
  };
};

/**
 * The step `startBrowserRenderTransaction` runs right after the render lock is
 * granted and before any OPFS work. Returns `{ok: false}` (outcome
 * `assets-changed`) when a source is no longer what the click saw, and rejects
 * (outcome `failure`) with `Audio file <label> cannot be used for rendering.`
 * when a source fails its preflight. Whatever it acquired is released on every
 * path that does not return `{ok: true}`.
 */
export const createAudioRenderPreparation =
  (input: AudioRenderPreparationInput) =>
  async (signal: AbortSignal): Promise<LocalAssetRenderPreparation> => {
    const {story, resolved, decodeCache} = input;
    const createObjectUrl =
      input.deps?.createObjectUrl ?? ((blob: Blob) => URL.createObjectURL(blob));
    const revokeObjectUrl =
      input.deps?.revokeObjectUrl ?? ((url: string) => URL.revokeObjectURL(url));

    const local: LocalAssetRenderPreparation =
      input.prepareLocal === null
        ? {ok: true, localAssetSources: undefined, release: () => undefined}
        : await input.prepareLocal(signal);

    if (!local.ok) {
      return local;
    }

    const urls: string[] = [];
    let released = false;
    const release = () => {
      if (released) {
        return;
      }

      released = true;

      for (const url of urls) {
        revokeObjectUrl(url);
      }

      local.release();
    };
    const labelOf = async (ref: AudioRef): Promise<string> => {
      if (isBundledAudio(ref)) {
        return bundledAudioCatalog[ref].label;
      }

      try {
        const row = await input.store?.getAssetRow(ref);

        return row?.status === "present" ? row.value.label : shortAssetId(ref);
      } catch {
        return shortAssetId(ref);
      }
    };

    try {
      const refs = storyAudioRefs(story);
      const states = input.getBundledStates();
      const bundledBlobs = new Map<BundledAudio, Blob>();

      // 1. Each referenced bundled id must still be ready; its blob is the one
      // the store checked in this session (the renderer never re-fetches it).
      for (const ref of refs) {
        if (!isBundledAudio(ref)) {
          continue;
        }

        const state = states.get(ref);

        if (state?.kind !== "ready" || !(state.blob instanceof Blob)) {
          release();

          return {ok: false, message: AUDIO_CHANGED_MESSAGE};
        }

        bundledBlobs.set(ref, state.blob);
      }

      signal.throwIfAborted();

      // 2. The frozen map: lease-owned local URLs plus render-owned bundled URLs.
      const bundledSources = new Map<BundledAudio, AudioSource>();

      for (const [id, blob] of bundledBlobs) {
        const url = createObjectUrl(blob);

        urls.push(url);
        bundledSources.set(id, {kind: "url", url});
      }

      const audioSources = Object.freeze(
        buildAudioSourceMap(story, local.localAssetSources, (id) =>
          bundledSources.get(id),
        ),
      );

      // 3. The durations the render will use: the verified local metadata
      // (the very payloads whose bytes the sources point at) and the catalog.
      const localDurations: Partial<Record<LocalAudioRef, number>> = {};

      for (const ref of refs) {
        if (isLocalAudioRef(ref)) {
          const verified = resolved?.verified.get(ref.slice(ref.length - 64));

          if (verified !== undefined && isAudioPayload(verified)) {
            localDurations[ref] = verified.durationSeconds;
          }
        }
      }

      const audioDurations: AudioDurationMap = storyAudioDurations(
        story,
        localDurations,
      );

      // 4. Re-check the frozen map: not one source may be pending or missing
      // (for example a bundled id that turned unusable after the click).
      const plan = compileFrozenAudioPlan(story, audioDurations, audioSources);

      if (plan.issues.some((issue) => issue.kind === "story-too-long-for-audio")) {
        const frames = getStoryMetadata(story, VIDEO_FPS, audioDurations)
          .durationInFrames;

        release();

        return {
          ok: false,
          message: storyTooLongWithVoicesMessage(Math.ceil(frames / VIDEO_FPS)),
        };
      }

      if (
        plan.issues.some(
          (issue) =>
            issue.kind === "missing-audio" || issue.kind === "pending-audio",
        )
      ) {
        release();

        return {ok: false, message: AUDIO_CHANGED_MESSAGE};
      }

      // 5. Preflight, on the exact bytes the render will read (AINV-8).
      input.onStatus?.(CHECKING_AUDIO_MESSAGE);
      for (const [id, blob] of bundledBlobs) {
        signal.throwIfAborted();

        const inspected = await inspectAudio(blob);
        const expected = bundledAudioCatalog[id].durationSeconds;

        if (
          !inspected.ok ||
          expected === null ||
          Math.abs(inspected.metadata.durationSeconds - expected) >
            BUNDLED_DURATION_TOLERANCE_SECONDS
        ) {
          throw new Error(audioFileUnusableMessage(await labelOf(id)));
        }
        // Its full decode is the store's: `ready` means inspected, decoded and
        // truncation-checked for exactly this Blob.
      }

      for (const ref of refs) {
        if (!isLocalAudioRef(ref)) {
          continue;
        }

        signal.throwIfAborted();

        const digest = ref.slice(ref.length - 64);
        const verified = resolved?.verified.get(digest);
        const blob = resolved?.blobs.get(digest);

        if (verified === undefined || blob === undefined || !isAudioPayload(verified)) {
          throw new Error(audioFileUnusableMessage(await labelOf(ref)));
        }

        // The local integrity check already compared the container metadata;
        // a decode in THIS page session is what proves the renderer will not stall.
        const decoded = await decodeCache.verify(digest, blob, verified, {signal});

        if (!decoded.ok) {
          throw new Error(audioFileUnusableMessage(await labelOf(ref)));
        }
      }

      signal.throwIfAborted();

      return {
        ok: true,
        localAssetSources: local.localAssetSources,
        audioSources,
        localAudioDurations: Object.freeze({...localDurations}),
        release,
      };
    } catch (error) {
      release();

      throw error;
    }
  };
```

### Task 11: `renderStoryMediaOnWeb` renders audio

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/web/browserRender.ts`, `tests/web-browser-render.test.mjs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 10 (`LocalAssetRenderPreparation.audioSources/localAudioDurations`, `compileFrozenAudioPlan`), Task 7 (`storyTooLongWithVoicesMessage`), `OUTPUT_AUDIO_BITRATE`, `OUTPUT_AUDIO_SAMPLE_RATE`.
- Produces: `AUDIO_DELAY_RENDER_TIMEOUT_MS = 30_000`; `renderStoryMediaOnWeb` options gain `audioSources?: AudioSourceMap` and `localAudioDurations?: AudioDurationMap`, and lose the test-only `bypassAudioBlockForTest` and the AUDIO-001 guard. With audio: `muted: false`, `audioCodec: "aac"`, `audioBitrate: 128000`, `sampleRate: 48000`, `delayRenderTimeoutInMilliseconds: 30000`, props `{story, localAssetSources, audioDurations, audioSources, bundledAudioPolicy: "explicit", audioArmed: true}` (the durations include `localAudioDurations`, so the composition length is the exact one); an audio ref without a resolved source (INV-5), including a ready local source without a measured duration and a bundled id without a map entry, throws `Cannot render: N audio source(s) have no resolved source.` before any OPFS work; an audio Story over 9,000 frames at its exact total throws `This Story is longer than 300 s with its voices (N s).`. Without audio the options and props are exactly the v0.3 ones. `startBrowserRenderTransaction` hands the preparation's `audioSources` and `localAudioDurations` to `renderStory`.
- Edits to existing tests: the describe `AUDIO-001 browser render uses the exact compiled timeline` (an auto voice without a measured duration rendered at the 3 s fallback; the cap message was the acceptance text) and the describe `AUDIO-001 interim render block (removed by AUDIO-005)` are replaced by `AUDIO-005 browser render with audio`.

```js
describe("AUDIO-001 browser render uses the exact compiled timeline", () => {
  const autoScene = {
    type: "intro",
    pose: "formal",
    background: "office",
    text: "Auto",
    duration: "auto",
    voice: {src: `local:audio:sha256:${"d".repeat(64)}`}, // no known duration: the 3 s fallback
  };
  const renderWith = async (story) => {
    let seen = null;

    await renderStoryMediaOnWeb(story, {
      signal: new AbortController().signal,
      bypassAudioBlockForTest: true,
      component: () => null,
      getDirectory: createFakeOpfs().getDirectory,
      render: async (options) => {
        seen = options;
        await finalizeOutput(options);

        return {getBlob: async () => new Blob(["v"]), internalState: {}};
      },
    });

    return seen;
  };

  it("an auto Story renders at the compiled total, not the acceptance lower bound", async () => {
    const story = {
      title: "Auto",
      scenes: [{...autoScene}, {...autoScene, text: "Two"}, {...exampleStory.scenes[0], duration: 2}],
    };
    const seen = await renderWith(story);

    // 2 auto scenes at the 3 s fallback (90 frames each) + one 2 s scene.
    assert.equal(seen.composition.durationInFrames, 90 + 90 + 60);
    assert.ok(seen.composition.durationInFrames > 16 + 16 + 60);
    assert.equal(
      seen.inputProps.audioDurations,
      seen.composition.defaultProps.audioDurations,
    );
    assert.notEqual(seen.inputProps.audioDurations, undefined);
    assert.equal(seen.inputProps.audioArmed, true);
    assert.equal(seen.composition.defaultProps.audioArmed, true);
  });

  it("a Story without audio keeps its duration and its props", async () => {
    const seen = await renderWith(exampleStory);

    assert.equal(seen.composition.durationInFrames, 360);
    assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(seen.inputProps), ["story", "localAssetSources"]);
    assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(seen.composition.defaultProps), [
      "story",
      "localAssetSources",
    ]);
    assert.equal("audioArmed" in seen.inputProps, false);
  });

  it("refuses an auto Story whose exact total exceeds the cap while its lower bound passes", async () => {
    const story = {
      title: "Long auto",
      scenes: Array.from({length: 101}, (_, i) => ({...autoScene, text: `S${i}`})),
    };
    let rendered = false;
    let directoryRequested = false;

    await assert.rejects(
      renderStoryMediaOnWeb(story, {
        signal: new AbortController().signal,
        bypassAudioBlockForTest: true,
      component: () => null,
        getDirectory: async () => {
          directoryRequested = true;
          return createFakeOpfs().getDirectory();
        },
        render: async () => {
          rendered = true;
        },
      }),
      /Active Story failed the browser render policy recheck: Browser authoring supports at most 9,000 frames/,
    );
    assert.equal(rendered, false);
    assert.equal(directoryRequested, false);
  });
});

describe("AUDIO-001 interim render block (removed by AUDIO-005)", () => {
  const MESSAGE =
    "This Story uses audio (music, sfx, voice or duration: auto), which cannot be rendered yet. Remove the audio fields to render.";
  const refuse = async (story) => {
    const calls = [];

    await assert.rejects(
      renderStoryMediaOnWeb(story, {
        signal: new AbortController().signal,
        component: () => null,
        getDirectory: async () => {
          calls.push("getDirectory");
          return createFakeOpfs().getDirectory();
        },
        render: async () => {
          calls.push("render");
        },
      }),
      (error) => error.message === MESSAGE,
    );

    assert.deepEqual(calls, []);
  };

  it("refuses a Story with music, sfx, voice or an auto duration before any OPFS or render work", async () => {
    const withMusic = structuredClone(exampleStory);
    withMusic.music = {src: "calm-loop"};
    const withSfx = structuredClone(exampleStory);
    withSfx.scenes[0].sfx = {src: "ding"};
    const withVoice = structuredClone(exampleStory);
    withVoice.scenes[0].voice = {src: "ding"};
    const withAuto = structuredClone(exampleStory);
    withAuto.scenes[0].duration = "auto";

    for (const story of [withMusic, withSfx, withVoice, withAuto]) {
      await refuse(story);
    }
  });

  it("still renders a Story without audio", async () => {
    let rendered = false;

    await renderStoryMediaOnWeb(exampleStory, {
      signal: new AbortController().signal,
      component: () => null,
      getDirectory: createFakeOpfs().getDirectory,
      render: async (options) => {
        rendered = true;
        await finalizeOutput(options);

        return {getBlob: async () => new Blob(["v"]), internalState: {}};
      },
    });

    assert.equal(rendered, true);
  });
});
```

```js
describe("AUDIO-005 browser render with audio", () => {
  const VOICE = `local:audio:sha256:${"d".repeat(64)}`;
  const OTHER_VOICE = `local:audio:sha256:${"e".repeat(64)}`;
  const autoScene = (voice = VOICE) => ({
    type: "intro",
    pose: "formal",
    background: "office",
    text: "Auto",
    duration: "auto",
    voice: {src: voice},
  });
  const voiceSources = (...refs) =>
    Object.fromEntries(refs.map((ref) => [ref, {kind: "url", url: `blob:voice/${ref.slice(-4)}`}]));
  const renderWith = async (story, options = {}) => {
    let seen = null;

    await renderStoryMediaOnWeb(story, {
      signal: new AbortController().signal,
      component: () => null,
      getDirectory: createFakeOpfs().getDirectory,
      render: async (renderOptions) => {
        seen = renderOptions;
        await finalizeOutput(renderOptions);

        return {getBlob: async () => new Blob(["v"]), internalState: {}};
      },
      ...options,
    });

    return seen;
  };
  const refuses = async (story, options, pattern) => {
    let rendered = false;
    let directoryRequested = false;

    await assert.rejects(
      renderStoryMediaOnWeb(story, {
        signal: new AbortController().signal,
        component: () => null,
        getDirectory: async () => {
          directoryRequested = true;

          return createFakeOpfs().getDirectory();
        },
        render: async () => {
          rendered = true;
        },
        ...options,
      }),
      pattern,
    );
    assert.equal(rendered, false);
    assert.equal(directoryRequested, false);
  };

  it("an auto Story renders at the compiled total with the measured voice duration, not the lower bound", async () => {
    const story = {
      title: "Auto",
      scenes: [autoScene(), autoScene(OTHER_VOICE), {...exampleStory.scenes[0], duration: 2}],
    };
    const seen = await renderWith(story, {
      audioSources: voiceSources(VOICE, OTHER_VOICE),
      localAudioDurations: {[VOICE]: 2.2, [OTHER_VOICE]: 2.2},
    });

    // Two auto scenes of ceil((2.2 + 0.5) x 30) = 81 frames + one 2 s scene.
    assert.equal(seen.composition.durationInFrames, 81 + 81 + 60);
    assert.ok(seen.composition.durationInFrames > 16 + 16 + 60);
    assert.equal(seen.inputProps.audioDurations, seen.composition.defaultProps.audioDurations);
    assert.deepEqual(seen.inputProps.audioDurations, {[VOICE]: 2.2, [OTHER_VOICE]: 2.2});
    assert.equal(seen.inputProps.audioArmed, true);
    assert.equal(seen.composition.defaultProps.audioArmed, true);
    assert.equal(seen.inputProps.bundledAudioPolicy, "explicit");
    assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(seen.inputProps.audioSources), [VOICE, OTHER_VOICE]);
  });

  it("muted and the audio options follow storyHasAudio", async () => {
    const withAudio = await renderWith(
      {title: "Ding", scenes: [{...exampleStory.scenes[0], sfx: {src: "ding"}}]},
      {audioSources: {ding: {kind: "url", url: "blob:render/ding"}}},
    );
    const silent = await renderWith(exampleStory);

    assert.equal(withAudio.muted, false);
    assert.equal(withAudio.audioCodec, "aac");
    assert.equal(withAudio.audioBitrate, 128_000);
    assert.equal(withAudio.sampleRate, 48_000);
    assert.equal(withAudio.delayRenderTimeoutInMilliseconds, 30_000);
    assert.equal(AUDIO_DELAY_RENDER_TIMEOUT_MS, 30_000);
    assert.equal(withAudio.inputProps.audioSources.ding.url, "blob:render/ding");

    // AINV-6: a silent Story is exactly the v0.3 render.
    assert.equal(silent.muted, true);
    for (const key of ["audioCodec", "audioBitrate", "sampleRate", "delayRenderTimeoutInMilliseconds"]) {
      assert.equal(key in silent, false, key);
    }
    assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(silent.inputProps), ["story", "localAssetSources"]);
    assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(silent.composition.defaultProps), ["story", "localAssetSources"]);
    assert.equal(silent.composition.durationInFrames, 360);
  });

  it("refuses a local or bundled audio ref that has no resolved source (INV-5), before any OPFS work", async () => {
    const voiceStory = {title: "Voice", scenes: [autoScene()]};
    const dingStory = {title: "Ding", scenes: [{...exampleStory.scenes[0], sfx: {src: "ding"}}]};
    const NO_SOURCE = /^Error: Cannot render: 1 audio source\(s\) have no resolved source\.$/u;

    // No audioSources at all.
    await refuses(voiceStory, {localAudioDurations: {[VOICE]: 2.2}}, NO_SOURCE);
    // A pending entry, an unavailable entry, a ready source whose duration was never measured.
    await refuses(
      voiceStory,
      {audioSources: {[VOICE]: {kind: "pending"}}, localAudioDurations: {[VOICE]: 2.2}},
      NO_SOURCE,
    );
    await refuses(
      voiceStory,
      {audioSources: {[VOICE]: {kind: "unavailable"}}, localAudioDurations: {[VOICE]: 2.2}},
      NO_SOURCE,
    );
    await refuses(voiceStory, {audioSources: voiceSources(VOICE)}, NO_SOURCE);
    // A bundled id resolves only through the map ("explicit"): no entry is pending, not a fallback to staticFile.
    await refuses(dingStory, {audioSources: {}}, NO_SOURCE);
    await refuses(dingStory, {audioSources: {ding: {kind: "unavailable"}}}, NO_SOURCE);

    // Two refs missing are counted once each.
    await refuses(
      {title: "Two", scenes: [autoScene(), autoScene(OTHER_VOICE)]},
      {localAudioDurations: {[VOICE]: 2.2, [OTHER_VOICE]: 2.2}},
      /^Error: Cannot render: 2 audio source\(s\) have no resolved source\.$/u,
    );
  });

  it("renders when every audio ref resolves, whatever mix of local and bundled sources", async () => {
    const story = {
      title: "Mixed",
      scenes: [{...autoScene(), sfx: {src: "ding"}}],
      music: {src: "whoosh"},
    };
    const seen = await renderWith(story, {
      audioSources: {
        ...voiceSources(VOICE),
        ding: {kind: "url", url: "blob:render/ding"},
        whoosh: {kind: "url", url: "blob:render/whoosh"},
      },
      localAudioDurations: {[VOICE]: 2.2},
    });

    assert.notEqual(seen, null);
  });

  it("an audio Story over the browser cap at its exact total says so with the voices sentence, and a silent Story keeps the policy text", async () => {
    // 101 auto scenes of ceil((3 + 0.5) x 30) = 105 frames: 10,605 frames, 353.5 s; the acceptance lower bound (101 x 16) passed.
    const story = {title: "Long auto", scenes: Array.from({length: 101}, () => autoScene())};

    await refuses(
      story,
      {audioSources: voiceSources(VOICE), localAudioDurations: {[VOICE]: 3}},
      /^Error: This Story is longer than 300 s with its voices \(354 s\)\.$/u,
    );

    const silent = {title: "Long", scenes: Array.from({length: 101}, () => ({...exampleStory.scenes[0], duration: 3}))};

    await refuses(
      silent,
      {},
      /Active Story failed the browser render policy recheck: Browser authoring supports at most 9,000 frames/u,
    );
  });

  it("the transaction hands the preparation's audio sources and local durations to the render, then releases", async () => {
    const calls = [];
    const story = {title: "Voice", scenes: [autoScene()]};
    const sources = voiceSources(VOICE);
    const outcome = await startBrowserRenderTransaction(story, {
      signal: new AbortController().signal,
      checkCapability: readyCapability,
      acquireLock: async () => ({mode: "owner", release: () => calls.push("lock-release")}),
      cleanup: async () => ({ok: true, attempts: 1}),
      prepareLocalAssets: async () => ({
        ok: true,
        localAssetSources: undefined,
        audioSources: sources,
        localAudioDurations: {[VOICE]: 2.2},
        release: () => calls.push("release"),
      }),
      renderStory: async (_story, options) => {
        calls.push(["render", options.audioSources, options.localAudioDurations]);

        return {getBlob: async () => new Blob(["mp4"]), internalState: {}};
      },
      materializeBlob: async (blob) => blob,
    });

    assert.equal(outcome.kind, "success");
    assert.deepEqual(calls, [
      ["render", sources, {[VOICE]: 2.2}],
      "lock-release",
      "release",
    ]);
  });
});
```

### Task 12: The post-render MP4 check

**Files:**
- Create: `src/web/renderedMp4Check.ts`, `tests/rendered-mp4-check.test.mjs`
- Modify: `src/web/browserRender.ts`, `tests/web-browser-render.test.mjs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: mediabunny `Input` + `BlobSource` + `ALL_FORMATS`, `OUTPUT_AUDIO_BITRATE`, `OUTPUT_AUDIO_SAMPLE_RATE`, the transaction's `materializeBlob` / `consumeBlob`.
- Produces:
  - `src/web/renderedMp4Check.ts`: `AAC_LC_CODEC_STRING = "mp4a.40.2"`, types `Mp4Facts`, `RenderedMp4Expectation = {frames; fps; width; height; hasAudio}`; `readMp4Facts(blob): Promise<Mp4Facts>`; `checkMp4Facts(facts, expected): string | null` (one video track, `avc`, the size, `packetCount === frames`, packet rate within 0.5 of `fps`; with audio exactly one audio track whose codec string is exactly `mp4a.40.2` (HE-AAC fails), 48 kHz, 2 channels, an average bitrate above 0 from the packet statistics (no range: the WASM AAC encoder ignores the requested bitrate, accepted), audio duration within `1 / fps + 60 ms` of the video; without audio no audio track); `verifyRenderedMp4(blob, expected, deps?): Promise<void>` (throws `The rendered MP4 is not the planned one: <problem>.`).
  - `startBrowserRenderTransaction` option `verifyOutput?: (blob, expected) => Promise<void>`: for a Story with audio (default `verifyRenderedMp4`) it runs on the materialized Blob before `consumeBlob`, with `frames` = the plan's length from the preparation's durations; a rejection is a `failure`. A Story without audio is never checked.
- Edits to existing tests: Task 11's transaction test passes `verifyOutput: async () => undefined` (its fake MP4 bytes are not an MP4).

```js
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import {mkdtemp, readFile, rm} from "node:fs/promises";
import {tmpdir} from "node:os";
import {join} from "node:path";
import {after, before, describe, it} from "node:test";
import {fileURLToPath} from "node:url";
import {
  AAC_LC_CODEC_STRING,
  checkMp4Facts,
  readMp4Facts,
  verifyRenderedMp4,
} from "../src/web/renderedMp4Check.ts";
import {runFfmpeg} from "./helpers/ffmpeg.mjs";

const EXPECTED = {frames: 60, fps: 30, width: 1080, height: 1920, hasAudio: true};
const goodFacts = () => ({
  videoTracks: [
    {codec: "avc", width: 1080, height: 1920, packetCount: 60, packetRate: 30, duration: 2},
  ],
  audioTracks: [
    {codecString: "mp4a.40.2", sampleRate: 48_000, channels: 2, averageBitrate: 128_000, duration: 2.02},
  ],
});
const withAudio = (patch) => {
  const facts = goodFacts();

  facts.audioTracks[0] = {...facts.audioTracks[0], ...patch};

  return facts;
};
const withVideo = (patch) => {
  const facts = goodFacts();

  facts.videoTracks[0] = {...facts.videoTracks[0], ...patch};

  return facts;
};

describe("checkMp4Facts: the planned MP4 of a Story with audio", () => {
  it("accepts H.264 1080x1920 30 FPS with the planned frame count and AAC-LC 48 kHz stereo at about 128 kb/s", () => {
    assert.equal(AAC_LC_CODEC_STRING, "mp4a.40.2");
    assert.equal(checkMp4Facts(goodFacts(), EXPECTED), null);
  });

  it("rejects HE-AAC (mp4a.40.5 and mp4a.40.29)", () => {
    for (const codecString of ["mp4a.40.5", "mp4a.40.29"]) {
      assert.match(
        checkMp4Facts(withAudio({codecString}), EXPECTED),
        new RegExp(`its audio is ${codecString.replaceAll(".", "\\.")}, not AAC-LC`, "u"),
      );
    }

    assert.match(checkMp4Facts(withAudio({codecString: null}), EXPECTED), /unknown, not AAC-LC/u);
  });

  it("only requires the audio to carry data: any bitrate above 0 passes (the WASM AAC encoder writes 67-89 kb/s, accepted by D-13)", () => {
    for (const averageBitrate of [1, 64_000, 67_000, 89_000, 128_000, 317_000]) {
      assert.equal(checkMp4Facts(withAudio({averageBitrate}), EXPECTED), null, String(averageBitrate));
    }

    for (const averageBitrate of [0, -1, Number.NaN]) {
      assert.match(
        checkMp4Facts(withAudio({averageBitrate}), EXPECTED),
        /its audio track carries no data/u,
        String(averageBitrate),
      );
    }
  });

  it("rejects another sample rate, mono, and an audio that ends more than a frame plus 60 ms after the video", () => {
    assert.match(checkMp4Facts(withAudio({sampleRate: 44_100}), EXPECTED), /44100 Hz, not 48000 Hz/u);
    assert.match(checkMp4Facts(withAudio({channels: 1}), EXPECTED), /1 channels, not stereo/u);
    assert.equal(checkMp4Facts(withAudio({duration: 2 + 1 / 30 + 0.05}), EXPECTED), null);
    assert.match(checkMp4Facts(withAudio({duration: 2 + 1 / 30 + 0.07}), EXPECTED), /long and its video/u);
    assert.match(checkMp4Facts(withAudio({duration: 1.8}), EXPECTED), /long and its video/u);
  });

  it("keeps the video checks: codec, size, frame count, frame rate", () => {
    assert.match(checkMp4Facts(withVideo({codec: "vp9"}), EXPECTED), /vp9, not H\.264/u);
    assert.match(checkMp4Facts(withVideo({width: 1920, height: 1080}), EXPECTED), /1920x1080, not 1080x1920/u);
    assert.match(checkMp4Facts(withVideo({packetCount: 59}), EXPECTED), /59 frames, not 60/u);
    assert.match(checkMp4Facts(withVideo({packetRate: 24}), EXPECTED), /24\.00 FPS, not 30/u);
  });

  it("rejects a missing audio track, a second audio track and a second video track", () => {
    const none = {...goodFacts(), audioTracks: []};
    const two = {...goodFacts(), audioTracks: [goodFacts().audioTracks[0], goodFacts().audioTracks[0]]};
    const videos = {...goodFacts(), videoTracks: [goodFacts().videoTracks[0], goodFacts().videoTracks[0]]};

    assert.match(checkMp4Facts(none, EXPECTED), /0 audio tracks, not 1/u);
    assert.match(checkMp4Facts(two, EXPECTED), /2 audio tracks, not 1/u);
    assert.match(checkMp4Facts(videos, EXPECTED), /2 video tracks, not 1/u);
  });

  it("without audio the check is the v0.3 one: no audio track", () => {
    const silent = {...EXPECTED, hasAudio: false};

    assert.equal(checkMp4Facts({...goodFacts(), audioTracks: []}, silent), null);
    assert.match(checkMp4Facts(goodFacts(), silent), /has an audio track and the Story has no audio/u);
  });
});

describe("verifyRenderedMp4", () => {
  it("resolves for the planned file and throws a sentence naming the problem otherwise", async () => {
    await verifyRenderedMp4(new Blob(["x"]), EXPECTED, {readFacts: async () => goodFacts()});
    await assert.rejects(
      verifyRenderedMp4(new Blob(["x"]), EXPECTED, {
        readFacts: async () => withAudio({codecString: "mp4a.40.5"}),
      }),
      /^Error: The rendered MP4 is not the planned one: its audio is mp4a\.40\.5, not AAC-LC \(mp4a\.40\.2\)\.$/u,
    );
  });
});

describe("readMp4Facts on real MP4 files", () => {
  let directory;
  const clip = {};

  before(async () => {
    directory = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "tora-mp4-check-"));

    // 2 s of the 1080x1920 background fixture at 30 FPS with a 440 Hz AAC track.
    for (const bitrate of ["128k", "64k"]) {
      clip[bitrate] = join(directory, `clip-${bitrate}.mp4`);
      await runFfmpeg([
        "-v", "error",
        "-loop", "1", "-framerate", "30", "-t", "2",
        "-i", fileURLToPath(new URL("./fixtures/local-assets/background-cyan.jpg", import.meta.url)),
        "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "sine=frequency=440:duration=2:sample_rate=48000",
        "-vf", "scale=1080:1920,format=yuv420p", "-r", "30",
        "-c:v", "libx264", "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", bitrate, "-ac", "2", "-shortest",
        "-y", clip[bitrate],
      ]);
    }
  });
  after(async () => {
    await rm(directory, {recursive: true, force: true});
  });

  it("reads the tracks of a 128 kb/s clip and accepts it as the planned file", async () => {
    const facts = await readMp4Facts(new Blob([await readFile(clip["128k"])]));

    assert.deepEqual(facts.videoTracks.map((v) => [v.codec, v.width, v.height, v.packetCount]), [
      ["avc", 1080, 1920, 60],
    ]);
    assert.equal(facts.audioTracks.length, 1);
    assert.equal(facts.audioTracks[0].codecString, "mp4a.40.2");
    assert.equal(facts.audioTracks[0].sampleRate, 48_000);
    assert.equal(facts.audioTracks[0].channels, 2);
    assert.ok(facts.audioTracks[0].averageBitrate > 110_000 && facts.audioTracks[0].averageBitrate < 150_000);
    assert.equal(checkMp4Facts(facts, EXPECTED), null);
  });

  it("accepts the real 64 kb/s clip (no bitrate range), and rejects a clip with another frame count", async () => {
    const facts = await readMp4Facts(new Blob([await readFile(clip["64k"])]));

    assert.ok(facts.audioTracks[0].averageBitrate > 50_000 && facts.audioTracks[0].averageBitrate < 80_000);
    assert.equal(checkMp4Facts(facts, EXPECTED), null);
    assert.match(
      checkMp4Facts(await readMp4Facts(new Blob([await readFile(clip["128k"])])), {...EXPECTED, frames: 90}),
      /60 frames, not 90/u,
    );
  });

  it("fails on bytes that are not an MP4", async () => {
    await assert.rejects(readMp4Facts(new Blob(["not an mp4 at all"])));
  });
});
```

```js
describe("AUDIO-005 the rendered MP4 is checked before it is handed over", () => {
  const VOICE = `local:audio:sha256:${"d".repeat(64)}`;
  const audioStory = {
    title: "Voice",
    scenes: [
      {
        type: "intro",
        pose: "formal",
        background: "office",
        text: "Auto",
        duration: "auto",
        voice: {src: VOICE},
      },
    ],
  };
  const transaction = (story, extra = {}) => {
    const calls = [];

    return {
      calls,
      run: () =>
        startBrowserRenderTransaction(story, {
          signal: new AbortController().signal,
          checkCapability: readyCapability,
          acquireLock: async () => ({mode: "owner", release: () => calls.push("lock-release")}),
          cleanup: async () => {
            calls.push("cleanup");

            return {ok: true, attempts: 1};
          },
          prepareLocalAssets: async () => ({
            ok: true,
            localAssetSources: undefined,
            audioSources: {[VOICE]: {kind: "url", url: "blob:voice"}},
            localAudioDurations: {[VOICE]: 2.2},
            release: () => calls.push("release"),
          }),
          renderStory: async () => ({getBlob: async () => new Blob(["mp4"]), internalState: {}}),
          materializeBlob: async (blob) => blob,
          consumeBlob: () => {
            calls.push("download");
          },
          ...extra,
        }),
    };
  };

  it("hands the planned length and geometry to the check, then downloads", async () => {
    const seen = [];
    const {calls, run} = transaction(audioStory, {
      verifyOutput: async (blob, expected) => {
        seen.push([await blob.text(), expected]);
      },
    });
    const outcome = await run();

    assert.equal(outcome.kind, "success");
    // ceil((2.2 + 0.5) x 30) = 81 frames from the preparation's measured duration.
    assert.deepEqual(seen, [
      ["mp4", {frames: 81, fps: 30, width: 1080, height: 1920, hasAudio: true}],
    ]);
    // Pre-render cleanup, the download, post-render cleanup, then the lock and the preparation's lease.
    assert.deepEqual(calls, ["cleanup", "download", "cleanup", "lock-release", "release"]);
  });

  it("a rejected check is a failed render: nothing is downloaded and the storage is still cleaned", async () => {
    const {calls, run} = transaction(audioStory, {
      verifyOutput: async () => {
        throw new Error("The rendered MP4 is not the planned one: its audio is mp4a.40.5, not AAC-LC (mp4a.40.2).");
      },
    });
    const outcome = await run();

    assert.equal(outcome.kind, "failure");
    assert.match(outcome.error.message, /not AAC-LC/u);
    assert.deepEqual(calls, ["cleanup", "cleanup", "lock-release", "release"]);
  });

  it("a Story without audio is never checked", async () => {
    let checked = false;
    const {calls, run} = transaction(exampleStory, {
      prepareLocalAssets: undefined,
      verifyOutput: async () => {
        checked = true;
      },
    });
    const outcome = await run();

    assert.equal(outcome.kind, "success");
    assert.equal(checked, false);
    assert.deepEqual(calls, ["cleanup", "download", "cleanup", "lock-release"]);
  });

  it("without an injected check, a Story with audio is verified by verifyRenderedMp4", async () => {
    const source = await readFile(new URL("../src/web/browserRender.ts", import.meta.url), "utf8");

    assert.match(source, /options\.verifyOutput \?\? verifyRenderedMp4/u);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

Create `src/web/renderedMp4Check.ts`:

```ts
// The post-render check of a browser MP4 that has audio (AUDIO-005, AINV-9):
// read what the renderer really wrote and refuse to hand over a file that is
// not the planned one. The check runs on the materialized Blob, before the
// download starts. A Story without audio keeps the v0.3 behavior: no check here.

import {ALL_FORMATS, BlobSource, Input} from "mediabunny";
import {OUTPUT_AUDIO_SAMPLE_RATE} from "../audio/constants.ts";

/** AAC-LC. HE-AAC (`mp4a.40.5`, `mp4a.40.29`) fails. */
export const AAC_LC_CODEC_STRING = "mp4a.40.2";
/** The audio may end this much after the video beyond one frame (encoder priming and padding). */
export const AUDIO_DURATION_SLACK_SECONDS = 0.06;

export type Mp4VideoFacts = {
  codec: string | null;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  packetCount: number;
  packetRate: number;
  duration: number;
};

export type Mp4AudioFacts = {
  /** The full codec string of the track, e.g. `mp4a.40.2`. */
  codecString: string | null;
  sampleRate: number;
  channels: number;
  /** Audio packet bytes x 8 / audio duration, from the packet statistics. */
  averageBitrate: number;
  duration: number;
};

export type Mp4Facts = {
  videoTracks: Mp4VideoFacts[];
  audioTracks: Mp4AudioFacts[];
};

export type RenderedMp4Expectation = {
  /** The plan's total length in frames. */
  frames: number;
  fps: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  hasAudio: boolean;
};

export const readMp4Facts = async (blob: Blob): Promise<Mp4Facts> => {
  const input = new Input({source: new BlobSource(blob), formats: ALL_FORMATS});

  try {
    const videoTracks: Mp4VideoFacts[] = [];
    const audioTracks: Mp4AudioFacts[] = [];

    for (const track of await input.getVideoTracks()) {
      const stats = await track.computePacketStats();

      videoTracks.push({
        codec: await track.getCodec(),
        width: await track.getCodedWidth(),
        height: await track.getCodedHeight(),
        packetCount: stats.packetCount,
        packetRate: stats.averagePacketRate,
        duration: await track.computeDuration(),
      });
    }

    for (const track of await input.getAudioTracks()) {
      const stats = await track.computePacketStats();

      audioTracks.push({
        codecString: await track.getCodecParameterString(),
        sampleRate: await track.getSampleRate(),
        channels: await track.getNumberOfChannels(),
        averageBitrate: stats.averageBitrate,
        duration: await track.computeDuration(),
      });
    }

    return {videoTracks, audioTracks};
  } finally {
    input.dispose();
  }
};

/** The first thing wrong with the facts, as a sentence, or null when the file is the planned one. */
export const checkMp4Facts = (
  facts: Mp4Facts,
  expected: RenderedMp4Expectation,
): string | null => {
  if (facts.videoTracks.length !== 1) {
    return `it has ${facts.videoTracks.length} video tracks, not 1`;
  }

  const [video] = facts.videoTracks;

  if (video.codec !== "avc") {
    return `its video is ${video.codec ?? "unknown"}, not H.264`;
  }

  if (video.width !== expected.width || video.height !== expected.height) {
    return `its video is ${video.width}x${video.height}, not ${expected.width}x${expected.height}`;
  }

  if (video.packetCount !== expected.frames) {
    return `its video has ${video.packetCount} frames, not ${expected.frames}`;
  }

  if (Math.abs(video.packetRate - expected.fps) > 0.5) {
    return `its video runs at ${video.packetRate.toFixed(2)} FPS, not ${expected.fps}`;
  }

  if (!expected.hasAudio) {
    return facts.audioTracks.length === 0 ? null : "it has an audio track and the Story has no audio";
  }

  if (facts.audioTracks.length !== 1) {
    return `it has ${facts.audioTracks.length} audio tracks, not 1`;
  }

  const [audio] = facts.audioTracks;

  if (audio.codecString !== AAC_LC_CODEC_STRING) {
    return `its audio is ${audio.codecString ?? "unknown"}, not AAC-LC (${AAC_LC_CODEC_STRING})`;
  }

  if (audio.sampleRate !== OUTPUT_AUDIO_SAMPLE_RATE) {
    return `its audio is ${audio.sampleRate} Hz, not ${OUTPUT_AUDIO_SAMPLE_RATE} Hz`;
  }

  if (audio.channels !== 2) {
    return `its audio has ${audio.channels} channels, not stereo`;
  }

  // No bitrate range: the WASM AAC encoder ignores the requested 128 kb/s (measured
  // 67-89 kb/s) and D-13 accepts it. The track only has to carry data.
  if (!(audio.averageBitrate > 0)) {
    return "its audio track carries no data";
  }

  if (Math.abs(audio.duration - video.duration) > 1 / expected.fps + AUDIO_DURATION_SLACK_SECONDS) {
    return `its audio is ${audio.duration.toFixed(2)} s long and its video ${video.duration.toFixed(2)} s`;
  }

  return null;
};

/** Throws when the rendered Blob is not the planned MP4. */
export const verifyRenderedMp4 = async (
  blob: Blob,
  expected: RenderedMp4Expectation,
  deps: {readFacts?: (blob: Blob) => Promise<Mp4Facts>} = {},
): Promise<void> => {
  const problem = checkMp4Facts(
    await (deps.readFacts ?? readMp4Facts)(blob),
    expected,
  );

  if (problem !== null) {
    throw new Error(`The rendered MP4 is not the planned one: ${problem}.`);
  }
};
```

### Task 13: Switch it on in `App` and remove the AUDIO-001 interim block

This is the spec's task 5 for the browser: after it, Render MP4 follows the audio readiness, the interim block exists nowhere, and `describeLocalAssetRenderBlock` names audio when the unavailable refs are audio.

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/app-audio-render.test.mjs`
- Modify: `src/web/App.tsx`, `src/localAssets/readiness.ts`, `src/audio/storyAudio.ts`, `src/web/audioRenderReadiness.ts`, `src/web/localAssetState.ts`, `tests/local-asset-readiness.test.mjs`, `tests/local-asset-state.test.mjs`, `tests/browser/browser-rendering.spec.mjs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 6 to 12, AUDIO-004's `bundledStates` (App already has `const bundledStates = useBundledAudioStates();`), `getBundledAudioStates` (the snapshot taken at the click).
- Produces: `storyHasAnyLocalAssetRef(story): boolean` (`src/localAssets/readiness.ts`; images or `local:audio:`; `storyHasLocalAssetRefs` stays image-only because it drives persistence v2); `App`: `audioRenderBlock` is `evaluateAudioRenderReadiness({story: activeStory, audioDurations, localState: localAssets.state, bundledStates}).block` (memoized), the browser local-asset gate is `storyHasAnyLocalAssetRef(snapshot)`, a Story with audio gets `createAudioRenderPreparation(...)` around the local preparation (also for a bundled-only Story), whose `onStatus` puts `Checking audio…` in the render banner while the sources are inspected and decoded, the capability is one state per `storyHasAudio` value read through `createRenderCapabilityCache`, `audioDecodeCache.invalidate(message.digests)` runs wherever `integrityCache.invalidate(message.digests)` does, a capability of any kind other than `ready` shows its message, and the banner names a local-asset block before an audio block. `AUDIO_RENDER_BLOCK_MESSAGE`, `data-audio-block` and the old `audioRenderBlock` constant no longer exist. `describeLocalAssetRenderBlock` says `N audio file(s)` for unavailable audio refs (`N local asset(s)` for images, both joined with `and` when mixed). `evaluateAudioRenderReadiness` returns early with an empty plan for a Story without audio.

```js
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import {readFileSync, readdirSync, statSync} from "node:fs";
import {join} from "node:path";
import {describe, it} from "node:test";
import * as storyAudio from "../src/audio/storyAudio.ts";

// Node cannot import .tsx, so App's wiring is pinned as source contracts (the
// pure parts it calls are unit-tested in their own files, and the behavior in
// tests/browser/audio-rendering.spec.mjs under system Chrome).
const app = readFileSync("src/web/App.tsx", "utf8");
const walk = (directory) =>
  readdirSync(directory).flatMap((name) => {
    const path = join(directory, name);

    return statSync(path).isDirectory() ? walk(path) : [path];
  });

describe("App renders Stories with audio (AUDIO-005 task 5)", () => {
  it("the AUDIO-001 interim block is gone everywhere", () => {
    assert.equal("AUDIO_RENDER_BLOCK_MESSAGE" in storyAudio, false);

    const sources = [...walk("src"), ...walk("scripts")].filter((path) =>
      /\.(ts|tsx)$/u.test(path),
    );

    for (const path of sources) {
      const text = readFileSync(path, "utf8");

      assert.doesNotMatch(text, /AUDIO_RENDER_BLOCK_MESSAGE|bypassAudioBlockForTest|data-audio-block/u, path);
      assert.doesNotMatch(text, /cannot be rendered yet/u, path);
    }

    assert.doesNotMatch(app, /audioRenderBlock = storyHasAudio/u);
  });

  it("the audio block comes from the readiness function over the page's own inputs and the store's snapshot", () => {
    // App reads the store once (AUDIO-004's `bundledStates`) and feeds that snapshot to readiness.
    assert.equal((app.match(/useBundledAudioStates\(\)/gu) ?? []).length, 1);
    assert.match(app, /evaluateAudioRenderReadiness\(\{\s*story: activeStory,\s*audioDurations,\s*localState: localAssets\.state,\s*bundledStates,\s*\}\)/u);
    // The click never builds a source map for readiness: the button reads the block.
    assert.match(app, /audioRenderBlock !== null \|\|\s*localAssetRenderBlock !== null/u);
  });

  it("the local-asset gate is any local ref, images or audio", () => {
    assert.match(app, /const hasLocalRefs = storyHasAnyLocalAssetRef\(snapshot\)/u);
    assert.doesNotMatch(app, /storyHasLocalAssetRefs/u);
  });

  it("a Story with audio gets the audio preparation around the local one, with the store's snapshot taken at the click", () => {
    assert.match(app, /if \(storyHasAudio\(snapshot\)\) \{[\s\S]*?createAudioRenderPreparation\(\{[\s\S]*?resolved: resolvedLocalAssets,[\s\S]*?prepareLocal: prepareLocalAssets \?\? null,[\s\S]*?getBundledStates: getBundledAudioStates,[\s\S]*?decodeCache: audioDecodeCache,[\s\S]*?\}\);\s*\}\s*const outcome = await startBrowserRenderTransaction\(/u);
  });

  it("the banner says Checking audio while the sources are inspected and decoded", () => {
    assert.match(app, /onStatus: \(message\) =>\s*setRenderUi/u);
  });

  it("the decode cache is invalidated at the same two moments as the integrity cache", () => {
    assert.equal(
      (app.match(/integrityCache\.invalidate\(message\.digests\);\s*audioDecodeCache\.invalidate\(message\.digests\);/gu) ?? []).length,
      2,
    );
  });

  it("the capability is probed per storyHasAudio value and shown whatever its kind", () => {
    assert.match(app, /createRenderCapabilityCache\(\(\{hasAudio\}\) =>\s*checkBrowserRenderCapability\(\{hasAudio\}\)/u);
    assert.match(app, /capabilityCache\.get\(storyUsesAudio\)/u);
    assert.match(app, /renderCapability\.kind !== "ready"\s*\?\s*renderCapability\.message/u);
    assert.doesNotMatch(app, /setRenderCapability\(/u);
  });

  it("the banner names a local-asset problem before an audio one", () => {
    const local = app.indexOf("? localAssetRenderBlock");
    const audio = app.indexOf("? audioRenderBlock");

    assert.ok(local > 0 && audio > local);
  });
});
```

```js
test("storyHasAnyLocalAssetRef is true for a Story whose only local ref is audio, while storyHasLocalAssetRefs stays image-only", () => {
  const silent = storyOf([["formal", "office"]]);
  const voiceOnly = withScenes(silent, [{voice: {src: audioRefOf(1)}}]);
  const musicOnly = {...silent, music: {src: audioRefOf(2)}};
  const bundledOnly = withScenes(silent, [{sfx: {src: "ding"}, voice: {src: "boom"}}]);

  assert.equal(storyHasAnyLocalAssetRef(silent), false);
  assert.equal(storyHasAnyLocalAssetRef(bundledOnly), false);
  assert.equal(storyHasAnyLocalAssetRef(voiceOnly), true);
  assert.equal(storyHasAnyLocalAssetRef(musicOnly), true);
  assert.equal(storyHasAnyLocalAssetRef(storyOf([[poseRef(1), "office"]])), true);

  // It drives persistence version 2, so audio refs must not count.
  assert.equal(storyHasLocalAssetRefs(voiceOnly), false);
  assert.equal(storyHasLocalAssetRefs(musicOnly), false);
});
```

```js
});
```

```ts
/**
 * Any local ref, image or audio: the gate of the render paths (the browser
 * resolves and prepares local assets, the CLI stages them) once audio can be
 * rendered. `storyHasLocalAssetRefs` stays image-only: it drives persistence v2.
 */
export const storyHasAnyLocalAssetRef = (story: Story): boolean =>
  storyHasLocalAssetRefs(story) || collectAudioUsages(story).length > 0;

export type ImagePayloadMetadata = {
  /** Absent in every record this build writes and in every v0.3 record; a stored "image" is tolerated and dropped on read. */
  kind?: "image";
```

```ts
    case "resolved": {
      const unavailable = state.refs.filter(
        (entry) => entry.status !== "ready",
      ).length;

      return unavailable === 0
        ? null

```

*New:*

```ts
    case "resolved": {
      const unavailable = state.refs.filter(
        (entry) => entry.status !== "ready",
      );
      const audio = unavailable.filter(
        (entry) => entry.usage.category === "audio",
      ).length;
      const images = unavailable.length - audio;

      if (unavailable.length === 0) {
        return null;
      }

      // Images keep the v0.3 sentence; audio is named as audio, never as an "asset".
      const what =
        audio === 0
          ? `${images} local asset(s)`
          : images === 0
            ? `${audio} audio file(s)`
            : `${images} local asset(s) and ${audio} audio file(s)`;

      return `Browser render is blocked because ${what} are unavailable in this browser.`;
    }
  }
};
```
