---

### Task 14: Real renders under system Chrome, the shared integration and the documentation

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/helpers/audioIntegration.mjs`, `tests/browser/audio-rendering.spec.mjs`, `tests/audio-seed-fixtures.test.mjs`
- Modify: `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (`timeout-minutes: 15` becomes `25`: the new system-Chrome spec file takes about 42 s here (10 tests, one worker) and the new CLI renders add about 45 s (`test:cli-e2e` goes from about 14 s to about 60 s); the one `verify` job on a hosted runner is typically 2 to 3 times slower, so about 3 to 4 more minutes on top of the 15-minute budget of the existing steps), `tests/helpers/ffmpeg.mjs` (`probeStreams`), `tests/browser/helpers/seedAssetLibrary.mjs` (the truncated MP3 record), `tests/cli-local-assets.e2e.mjs`, `playwright.config.mjs`, `playwright.browser-render.config.mjs`, `README.md`, `local-assets/README.md`, `docs/audio/AUDIO-005-rendering.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything above, `audioWavRecord`, `deleteAssetRows`, `openSeeded`, `applyYaml`, `isBrowserRenderSupported`, `listRemotionOpfsEntries` (existing browser helpers), Task 1.
- Produces:
  - `tests/helpers/audioIntegration.mjs`: `INTEGRATION_VOICE_FIXTURE`, `integrationStoryYaml(voiceRef)` (a `ding` scene of 2 s, then a scene whose `auto` length comes from the imported 440 Hz 2.2 s voice: 141 frames), `INTEGRATION_FRAMES`, `assertIntegrationEnvelope(samples, label)` (Goertzel per 100 ms: the ding at its onset, 440 Hz only during the voice, silence after), `assertAacLcStereoStream(stream, label)`; one definition shared by the browser spec and the CLI e2e.
  - `tests/helpers/ffmpeg.mjs`: `probeStreams(path)` (ffprobe `-show_streams` as JSON).
  - `tests/browser/helpers/seedAssetLibrary.mjs`: `TRUNCATED_MP3_METADATA`, `truncatedMp3Record(options?)` (a seeded library record whose metadata parses and whose bytes decode to about 6 of the declared 10 s; `tests/audio-seed-fixtures.test.mjs` pins the numbers against `inspectAudio`).
  - `tests/browser/audio-rendering.spec.mjs` (system Chrome): bundled sfx (Render disabled with `Audio is still loading.` while `ding.wav` is held, enabled by itself when released, renders through `blob:` URLs with no request for the file after the click, one AAC-LC 48 kHz stereo track, at 128 kb/s +-10% only when the page's Chrome encodes AAC natively (`AudioEncoder.isConfigSupported` is asked in the page; the ubuntu Chrome of CI probably takes the accepted WASM path, which must stay green too), the ding audible at its onset); silent Story still video-only; the WASM-AAC path (native AAC forced off by an init script: the render succeeds and passes the check, bitrate not range-checked); deleted local voice blocks Render; the truncated MP3 fails the preflight quickly with nothing left in OPFS; imported voice + ding render together (shared envelope); `ding.wav` undecodable disables Render with the readiness message and starts nothing; a first network failure recovers by itself; four failures show `Retry loading Ding` and a click after fixing the route enables Render; an actual render of an audio Story locks every audio control.
  - CLI e2e additions: the shared integration Story with a local voice (CLI half) and the missing-voice refusal.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

The shared fixture and the helpers:

Create `tests/helpers/audioIntegration.mjs`:

```js
// The one Story and the one set of measurements that the browser render
// (tests/browser/audio-rendering.spec.mjs, system Chrome) and the CLI render
// (tests/cli-local-assets.e2e.mjs) share, so "both MP4s sound the same" is
// asserted against a single definition (AUDIO-005 integration; AUDIO-006
// builds its parity measurements on tests/helpers/audioEnvelope.mjs).
//
// The fixtures are pure tones: the imported voice is 440 Hz for 2.2 s, the
// bundled `ding` is 880 Hz and 1760 Hz partials with an exponential decay.

import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import {goertzelWindows} from "./audioEnvelope.mjs";

export const INTEGRATION_VOICE_FIXTURE = "tone-440-2.2s.wav";

/**
 * Scene 1: a ding at 0 s, 2 s long. Scene 2: the imported voice at 2 s, with
 * the scene length taken from the voice (2.2 s + 0.5 s = 81 frames).
 * Total: 141 frames = 4.7 s.
 */
export const integrationStoryYaml = (voiceRef) =>
  [
    "title: Audio integration",
    "scenes:",
    "  - type: intro",
    "    pose: formal",
    "    background: office",
    "    animation: fade",
    '    text: "Ding first."',
    "    sfx:",
    "      src: ding",
    "    duration: 2",
    "  - type: dialogue",
    "    pose: formal",
    "    background: office",
    "    animation: fade",
    '    text: "Then the voice."',
    "    voice:",
    `      src: ${voiceRef}`,
    "    duration: auto",
    "",
  ].join("\n");

export const INTEGRATION_FRAMES = 141;
export const INTEGRATION_SECONDS = INTEGRATION_FRAMES / 30;

const window100 = (samples, hz) => goertzelWindows(samples, hz, 100);
const max = (values) => Math.max(...values);

/**
 * What a render of `integrationStoryYaml` must sound like, measured on the
 * decoded mono samples of the MP4 (100 ms Goertzel windows, 48 kHz).
 */
export const assertIntegrationEnvelope = (samples, label) => {
  const seconds = samples.length / 48_000;
  const ding = window100(samples, 880);
  const dingPartial = window100(samples, 1760);
  const voice = window100(samples, 440);
  const message = (what) => `${label}: ${what}`;

  // Length: the Story's 4.7 s, with a little encoder padding.
  assert.ok(
    seconds >= INTEGRATION_SECONDS - 0.1 && seconds <= INTEGRATION_SECONDS + 0.2,
    message(`${seconds.toFixed(3)} s long`),
  );

  // The ding sounds at the first scene's start, fundamental and partial (positive control).
  assert.ok(ding[0] > 0.02 && ding[1] > 0.02, message(`ding at 0 s: ${ding.slice(0, 3)}`));
  assert.ok(
    dingPartial[0] > 0.01 && dingPartial[1] > 0.01,
    message(`ding partial at 0 s: ${dingPartial.slice(0, 3)}`),
  );
  // ...while the 440 Hz voice has not started: nothing at 440 Hz before 2 s.
  assert.ok(max(voice.slice(0, 19)) < 0.02, message(`440 Hz before the voice: ${max(voice.slice(0, 19))}`));

  // The ding has decayed before the voice starts, and does not return.
  assert.ok(max(ding.slice(15)) < 0.01, message(`880 Hz after 1.5 s: ${max(ding.slice(15))}`));

  // The voice is present for its 2.2 s, at about the fixture's level (windows 2.1 s - 4.1 s).
  for (const amplitude of voice.slice(21, 41)) {
    assert.ok(amplitude > 0.5, message(`voice windows: ${voice.slice(21, 41)}`));
  }

  // ...and silent after it ends (the scene's 0.5 s of padding).
  assert.ok(max(voice.slice(43)) < 0.02, message(`440 Hz after the voice: ${max(voice.slice(43))}`));
};

/**
 * AINV-9 on an ffprobe stream: AAC-LC, 48 kHz, stereo, and (native AAC and the
 * CLI) about 128 kb/s, +-10%. `{bitrate: false}` skips the bitrate range for the
 * WASM AAC encoder, which ignores the requested bitrate (D-13 accepts it): the
 * stream then only has to carry data. The repository ffprobe names the LC
 * profile "1", other builds "LC".
 */
export const assertAacLcStereoStream = (stream, label, {bitrate = true} = {}) => {
  assert.equal(stream.codec_type, "audio", label);
  assert.equal(stream.codec_name, "aac", label);
  assert.ok(["LC", "1"].includes(stream.profile), `${label}: profile ${stream.profile}`);
  assert.equal(Number(stream.sample_rate), 48_000, label);
  assert.equal(stream.channels, 2, label);

  const bitRate = Number(stream.bit_rate);

  if (bitrate) {
    assert.ok(bitRate >= 115_200 && bitRate <= 140_800, `${label}: ${bitRate} b/s`);
  } else {
    assert.ok(bitRate > 0, `${label}: ${bitRate} b/s`);
  }
};
```

Edit `tests/helpers/ffmpeg.mjs` (1 change; each *Old* text is the file's current text and occurs exactly once; replace it with *New*):

*Change 1 of 1 - Old:*

```js
  };
};

/** Samples per pixel of the 8-bit PNG colour types ffmpeg writes (RGB, RGBA). */
const PNG_CHANNELS = {2: 3, 6: 4};
```

*New:*

```js
  };
};

/**
 * Every stream of a media file as ffprobe reports it (`codec_type`,
 * `codec_name`, `profile`, `sample_rate`, `channels`, `bit_rate`, `duration`,
 * `nb_frames`...). The repository ffprobe prints the AAC profile as its number
 * (1 = LC) instead of its name.
 */
export const probeStreams = async (path) => {
  const {ffprobe, dir} = resolveFfmpegBinaries();

  try {
    const {stdout} = await run(ffprobe, dir, [
      "-v",
      "error",
      "-show_streams",
      "-of",
      "json",
      path,
    ]);

    return JSON.parse(stdout).streams ?? [];
  } catch (error) {
    throw new Error(
      `probeStreams could not read ${path}: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`,
    );
  }
};

/** Samples per pixel of the 8-bit PNG colour types ffmpeg writes (RGB, RGBA). */
const PNG_CHANNELS = {2: 3, 6: 4};
```

Edit `tests/browser/helpers/seedAssetLibrary.mjs` (1 change; each *Old* text is the file's current text and occurs exactly once; replace it with *New*):

*Change 1 of 1 - Old:*

    thumbnail: null,
  };
};
```

*New:*

    thumbnail: null,
  };
};

/**
 * What `inspectAudio` reports for tests/fixtures/local-audio/truncated.mp3: the
 * first 60% of the 10 s MP3, whose Xing header still declares the full length.
 * Its metadata parses (so the integrity check accepts the seeded record) but a
 * full decode yields only about 6 s, so only a decode in the page catches it.
 * tests/audio-seed-fixtures.test.mjs pins these numbers against `inspectAudio`.
 */
export const TRUNCATED_MP3_METADATA = {
  kind: "audio",
  mimeType: "audio/mpeg",
  container: "mp3",
  codec: "mp3",
  durationSeconds: 10.031020408163265,
  sampleRate: 44_100,
  channels: 2,
};

/** A seed record for the truncated MP3 fixture: readable metadata, bytes that do not decode completely. */
export const truncatedMp3Record = async (options = {}) => {
  const fixture = await readAudioFixture("truncated.mp3");

  return {
    ref: localAssetRef("audio", fixture.digest),
    digest: fixture.digest,
    meta: {...TRUNCATED_MP3_METADATA, byteSize: fixture.bytes.length},
    bytesBase64: fixture.base64,
    mimeType: "audio/mpeg",
    row: options.row !== false,
    label: options.label ?? "truncated",
    thumbnail: null,
  };
};
```

Create `tests/audio-seed-fixtures.test.mjs`:

```js
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import {describe, it} from "node:test";
import {audioMimeTypeFor, inspectAudio} from "../src/localAssets/audioInspection.ts";
import {fixtureBytes} from "./helpers/audioBytes.mjs";
import {
  audioWavRecord,
  TRUNCATED_MP3_METADATA,
  truncatedMp3Record,
} from "./browser/helpers/seedAssetLibrary.mjs";

describe("the audio records that the browser specs seed", () => {
  it("the truncated MP3 record carries exactly what inspectAudio reports, so the integrity check accepts it", async () => {
    const inspected = await inspectAudio(fixtureBytes("truncated.mp3"));

    assert.equal(inspected.ok, true);
    assert.deepEqual(TRUNCATED_MP3_METADATA, {
      kind: "audio",
      mimeType: audioMimeTypeFor(inspected.metadata.container),
      ...inspected.metadata,
    });

    const record = await truncatedMp3Record();

    assert.equal(record.meta.byteSize, fixtureBytes("truncated.mp3").length);
    assert.match(record.ref, /^local:audio:sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$/u);
  });

  it("the WAV record used by the render specs matches inspectAudio too (duration 2.2 s)", async () => {
    const record = await audioWavRecord("tone-440-2.2s.wav");
    const inspected = await inspectAudio(fixtureBytes("tone-440-2.2s.wav"));

    assert.equal(record.meta.durationSeconds, inspected.metadata.durationSeconds);
    assert.equal(record.meta.sampleRate, inspected.metadata.sampleRate);
  });
});
```

The system-Chrome spec. Its last test locks the editor during a real render and names AUDIO-004's controls; the other tests need only the readiness UI of Task 13:

Create `tests/browser/audio-rendering.spec.mjs`:

```js
import {expect, test} from "@playwright/test";
import {
  assertAacLcStereoStream,
  assertIntegrationEnvelope,
  INTEGRATION_FRAMES,
  INTEGRATION_VOICE_FIXTURE,
  integrationStoryYaml,
} from "../helpers/audioIntegration.mjs";
import {decodeMp4AudioMono, goertzelWindows} from "../helpers/audioEnvelope.mjs";
import {probeMp4, probeStreams} from "../helpers/ffmpeg.mjs";
import {
  applyYaml,
  isBrowserRenderSupported,
  listRemotionOpfsEntries,
  openSeeded,
  waitForOwner,
} from "./helpers/localAssetPage.mjs";
import {
  audioWavRecord,
  deleteAssetRows,
  truncatedMp3Record,
} from "./helpers/seedAssetLibrary.mjs";

// Browser MP4 render with audio (AUDIO-005). It needs H.264 and AAC encoding,
// which bundled Chromium cannot do, so this file runs under system Chrome
// (playwright.browser-render.config.mjs) and skips itself where rendering is
// unavailable.

const renderButton = (page) => page.getByRole("button", {name: "Render MP4"});
const banner = (page) => page.locator(".render-banner");

/**
 * Whether this Chrome encodes AAC natively (Windows, macOS). Where it does not
 * (the ubuntu Chrome of CI), the web renderer registers its WASM encoder, which
 * ignores the requested bitrate (D-13, accepted): the 128 kb/s range is then not asserted.
 */
const encodesAacNatively = (page) =>
  page.evaluate(
    async () =>
      (
        await AudioEncoder.isConfigSupported({
          codec: "mp4a.40.2",
          sampleRate: 48_000,
          numberOfChannels: 2,
          bitrate: 128_000,
        })
      ).supported,
  );

const requireRenderSupport = async (page) => {
  if (!(await isBrowserRenderSupported(page))) {
    test.skip(true, "Browser web rendering is unavailable in this runtime");
  }
};

const storyYaml = (scenes, title = "Audio render") =>
  [
    `title: ${title}`,
    "scenes:",
    ...scenes.flatMap(({sfx, voice, duration = 2}) => [
      "  - type: intro",
      "    pose: formal",
      "    background: office",
      "    animation: fade",
      '    text: "Audio render."',
      ...(sfx === undefined ? [] : ["    sfx:", `      src: ${sfx}`]),
      ...(voice === undefined ? [] : ["    voice:", `      src: ${voice}`]),
      `    duration: ${duration}`,
    ]),
    "",
  ].join("\n");

/** Records every fetch of the page (init script), so a render's sources can be told apart. */
const recordFetches = (page) =>
  page.addInitScript(() => {
    window.__fetchedUrls = [];

    const original = window.fetch.bind(window);

    window.fetch = (input, init) => {
      window.__fetchedUrls.push(String(input instanceof Request ? input.url : input));

      return original(input, init);
    };
  });
const fetchedUrls = (page) => page.evaluate(() => window.__fetchedUrls);

const renderAndDownload = async (page, timeout = 200_000) => {
  const downloadPromise = page.waitForEvent("download", {timeout});

  await renderButton(page).click();

  const download = await downloadPromise;
  const path = await download.path();

  if (path === null) {
    throw new Error("Expected a browser MP4 download path");
  }

  await expect(page.locator(".app-shell")).toHaveAttribute("data-render-state", "success", {
    timeout: 60_000,
  });

  return path;
};

test("a Story with a bundled sfx enables Render by itself, renders it through object URLs and downloads one AAC-LC 48 kHz stereo track", async ({
  page,
}) => {
  test.setTimeout(240_000);

  const dingRequests = [];
  let releaseDing = () => undefined;
  const dingGate = new Promise((resolve) => {
    releaseDing = resolve;
  });

  await recordFetches(page);
  await page.route("**/audio/sfx/ding.wav", async (route) => {
    await dingGate;
    await route.continue();
  });
  page.on("request", (request) => {
    if (request.url().endsWith("/audio/sfx/ding.wav")) {
      dingRequests.push(request.url());
    }
  });

  await page.goto("/", {waitUntil: "domcontentloaded"});
  await waitForOwner(page);
  await requireRenderSupport(page);
  await applyYaml(page, storyYaml([{sfx: "ding"}, {}]));

  // The sound is still loading: Render is blocked with the reason and nothing is prepared.
  await expect(renderButton(page)).toBeDisabled();
  await expect(banner(page)).toContainText("Audio is still loading.");
  await expect(page.locator(".app-shell")).toHaveAttribute("data-render-state", "idle");

  // The store's check succeeds: Render enables with no other interaction.
  releaseDing();
  await expect(renderButton(page)).toBeEnabled({timeout: 20_000});
  await expect(banner(page)).not.toContainText("Audio is still loading.");

  const requestsBefore = dingRequests.length;
  const fetchesBefore = (await fetchedUrls(page)).length;
  const path = await renderAndDownload(page);

  // The renderer reads render-owned object URLs, never the file on the server.
  expect(dingRequests.length).toBe(requestsBefore);

  const duringRender = (await fetchedUrls(page)).slice(fetchesBefore);

  expect(duringRender.some((url) => url.startsWith("blob:"))).toBe(true);
  expect(duringRender.some((url) => url.includes("/audio/sfx/"))).toBe(false);

  const streams = await probeStreams(path);
  const video = streams.filter((stream) => stream.codec_type === "video");
  const audio = streams.filter((stream) => stream.codec_type === "audio");

  expect(video).toHaveLength(1);
  expect(video[0].codec_name).toBe("h264");
  expect(Number(video[0].nb_frames)).toBe(120);
  expect(audio).toHaveLength(1);
  assertAacLcStereoStream(audio[0], "browser render", {
    bitrate: await encodesAacNatively(page),
  });

  // The ding is audible at the start and gone later (positive control + silence).
  const samples = await decodeMp4AudioMono(path);
  const ding = goertzelWindows(samples, 880, 100);

  expect(ding[0]).toBeGreaterThan(0.02);
  expect(Math.max(...ding.slice(20))).toBeLessThan(0.01);
  expect(await listRemotionOpfsEntries(page)).toEqual([]);
});

test("a Story without audio still produces a video-only MP4", async ({page}) => {
  test.setTimeout(180_000);

  await page.goto("/", {waitUntil: "domcontentloaded"});
  await waitForOwner(page);
  await requireRenderSupport(page);
  await applyYaml(page, storyYaml([{duration: 1}], "Silent render"));
  await expect(renderButton(page)).toBeEnabled();

  const path = await renderAndDownload(page);
  const info = await probeMp4(path);

  expect(info.hasAudio).toBe(false);
  expect(info.videoStreams).toBe(1);
  expect(info.codec).toBe("h264");
  expect(info.frames).toBe(30);
});

test("without a native AAC encoder the WASM encoder renders and the file passes the check", async ({
  page,
}) => {
  test.setTimeout(240_000);

  // The browser says it cannot encode AAC, so the web renderer registers its
  // WASM encoder (D-13). MEASURED (Chrome 155, @remotion/web-renderer 4.0.529):
  // it writes AAC-LC 48 kHz stereo but ignores the requested bitrate (67 kb/s
  // for the ding story, 89 kb/s for the integration story); that is accepted, so
  // the post-render check does not look at the bitrate and the render succeeds.
  await page.addInitScript(() => {
    const original = AudioEncoder.isConfigSupported.bind(AudioEncoder);

    AudioEncoder.isConfigSupported = async (config) =>
      String(config.codec).startsWith("mp4a")
        ? {supported: false, config}
        : original(config);
  });
  await page.goto("/", {waitUntil: "domcontentloaded"});
  await waitForOwner(page);
  await requireRenderSupport(page);
  await applyYaml(page, storyYaml([{sfx: "ding", duration: 2}], "Wasm AAC"));
  await expect(renderButton(page)).toBeEnabled({timeout: 30_000});

  const path = await renderAndDownload(page);
  const audio = (await probeStreams(path)).filter((stream) => stream.codec_type === "audio");

  expect(audio).toHaveLength(1);
  assertAacLcStereoStream(audio[0], "wasm aac", {bitrate: false});

  // The sound is really there (positive control).
  const ding = goertzelWindows(await decodeMp4AudioMono(path), 880, 100);

  expect(ding[0]).toBeGreaterThan(0.02);
  expect(await listRemotionOpfsEntries(page)).toEqual([]);
});

test("a Story whose local voice was deleted cannot render", async ({page}) => {
  test.setTimeout(120_000);

  const voice = await audioWavRecord(INTEGRATION_VOICE_FIXTURE, {label: "Voice take"});

  await openSeeded(page, [voice]);
  await requireRenderSupport(page);
  await applyYaml(page, storyYaml([{voice: voice.ref, duration: 3}], "Deleted voice"));
  await expect(renderButton(page)).toBeEnabled({timeout: 20_000});

  await deleteAssetRows(page, [voice.ref]);
  await page.evaluate(() => window.dispatchEvent(new Event("focus")));

  await expect(renderButton(page)).toBeDisabled();
  await expect(banner(page)).toContainText(
    "Browser render is blocked because 1 audio file(s) are unavailable in this browser.",
  );
});

test("a seeded local audio file whose metadata parses but whose samples do not decode fails the preflight before OPFS is touched", async ({
  page,
}) => {
  test.setTimeout(120_000);

  const truncated = await truncatedMp3Record({label: "Truncated take"});

  await openSeeded(page, [truncated]);
  await requireRenderSupport(page);
  await applyYaml(page, storyYaml([{voice: truncated.ref, duration: 3}], "Truncated voice"));
  // Readiness does not decode: the metadata is readable, so the button is enabled.
  await expect(renderButton(page)).toBeEnabled({timeout: 20_000});

  const started = Date.now();

  await renderButton(page).click();
  await expect(page.locator(".app-shell")).toHaveAttribute("data-render-state", "failure", {
    timeout: 15_000,
  });
  await expect(banner(page)).toContainText(
    "Browser render failed: Audio file Truncated take cannot be used for rendering.",
  );

  // Quickly, not after a stalled render, with nothing left behind and authoring unlocked.
  expect(Date.now() - started).toBeLessThan(15_000);
  expect(await listRemotionOpfsEntries(page)).toEqual([]);
  await expect(page.locator(".app-shell")).toHaveAttribute("data-authoring-locked", "false");
});

test("an imported voice and a bundled ding render together in the browser: 440 Hz during the voice and the ding at its onset", async ({
  page,
}) => {
  test.setTimeout(240_000);

  const voice = await audioWavRecord(INTEGRATION_VOICE_FIXTURE, {label: "Voice take"});

  await recordFetches(page);
  await openSeeded(page, [voice]);
  await requireRenderSupport(page);
  await applyYaml(page, integrationStoryYaml(voice.ref));
  await expect(renderButton(page)).toBeEnabled({timeout: 30_000});

  const fetchesBefore = (await fetchedUrls(page)).length;
  const path = await renderAndDownload(page);

  // A lease blob: URL for the voice and a render-owned blob: URL for ding: two different objects, no server fetch.
  const duringRender = (await fetchedUrls(page)).slice(fetchesBefore);
  const blobUrls = new Set(duringRender.filter((url) => url.startsWith("blob:")));

  expect(blobUrls.size).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);
  expect(duringRender.some((url) => url.includes("/audio/sfx/"))).toBe(false);

  const info = await probeMp4(path);

  expect(info.frames).toBe(INTEGRATION_FRAMES);
  assertIntegrationEnvelope(await decodeMp4AudioMono(path), "browser render");
});

const dingStory = () => storyYaml([{sfx: "ding", duration: 2}], "Ding readiness");
const DING = "**/audio/sfx/ding.wav";

test("a ding.wav that cannot be decoded blocks Render with the readiness message as soon as the decode fails, and no render starts", async ({
  page,
}) => {
  test.setTimeout(120_000);

  await page.route(DING, (route) =>
    route.fulfill({status: 200, contentType: "audio/wav", body: Buffer.from("RIFF not really a wav")}),
  );
  await page.goto("/", {waitUntil: "domcontentloaded"});
  await waitForOwner(page);
  await requireRenderSupport(page);
  await applyYaml(page, dingStory());

  await expect(banner(page)).toContainText("Audio file ding cannot be used for rendering.", {
    timeout: 15_000,
  });
  await expect(renderButton(page)).toBeDisabled();
  // No other interaction, and nothing was started.
  await expect(page.locator(".app-shell")).toHaveAttribute("data-render-state", "idle");
  expect(await listRemotionOpfsEntries(page)).toEqual([]);
});

test("a first network failure of ding.wav is retried by itself and Render enables without a reload", async ({
  page,
}) => {
  test.setTimeout(120_000);

  let requests = 0;

  await page.route(DING, (route) => {
    requests += 1;

    return requests === 1 ? route.abort("failed") : route.continue();
  });
  await page.goto("/", {waitUntil: "domcontentloaded"});
  await waitForOwner(page);
  await requireRenderSupport(page);
  await applyYaml(page, dingStory());

  // The first request fails; the store retries after 1 s with no navigation, select or Story change.
  await expect(renderButton(page)).toBeEnabled({timeout: 20_000});
  await expect(banner(page)).not.toContainText("Audio");
  expect(requests).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);
});

test("when every request for ding.wav fails, Retry loading Ding appears and clicking it after the route is fixed enables Render", async ({
  page,
}) => {
  test.setTimeout(120_000);

  let failing = true;

  await page.route(DING, (route) => (failing ? route.abort("failed") : route.continue()));
  await page.goto("/", {waitUntil: "domcontentloaded"});
  await waitForOwner(page);
  await requireRenderSupport(page);
  await applyYaml(page, dingStory());

  // The Retry button belongs to the Sound effect field of the visual editor.
  await page.getByRole("button", {name: "Open visual editor"}).click();

  // 1 s + 2 s + 4 s of automatic retries, then the manual one.
  const retry = page.getByRole("button", {name: "Retry loading Ding"});

  await expect(retry).toBeVisible({timeout: 30_000});
  await expect(banner(page)).toContainText("Audio could not be loaded. Retry it in the editor.");
  await expect(renderButton(page)).toBeDisabled();

  failing = false;
  await retry.click();
  await expect(renderButton(page)).toBeEnabled({timeout: 20_000});
  await expect(banner(page)).not.toContainText("Audio");
});

test("during an actual render of an audio Story the editor is locked: every audio control, the My audio controls and Caption are disabled", async ({
  page,
}) => {
  test.setTimeout(240_000);

  const voice = await audioWavRecord(INTEGRATION_VOICE_FIXTURE, {label: "Voice take"});

  await openSeeded(page, [voice]);
  await requireRenderSupport(page);
  await applyYaml(
    page,
    [
      "title: Locked render",
      "music:",
      "  src: ding",
      "scenes:",
      "  - type: intro",
      "    pose: formal",
      "    background: office",
      "    text: One",
      "    sfx:",
      "      src: boom",
      "    voice:",
      `      src: ${voice.ref}`,
      "    duration: 12",
      "",
    ].join("\n"),
  );
  await page.getByRole("button", {name: "Open visual editor"}).click();

  const controls = [
    page.getByLabel("Caption"),
    page.getByRole("combobox", {name: "Music", exact: true}),
    page.getByLabel("Music volume"),
    page.getByLabel("Music ducking"),
    page.getByLabel("Fade in (seconds)"),
    page.getByLabel("Fade out (seconds)"),
    page.getByRole("combobox", {name: "Sound effect", exact: true}),
    page.getByLabel("Sound effect volume"),
    page.getByRole("combobox", {name: "Voice", exact: true}),
    page.getByLabel("Voice volume"),
    page.getByLabel("Auto (from voice)"),
    page.locator('[data-my-audio] input[type="file"]'),
    page.getByRole("button", {name: "Play Voice take"}),
  ];

  await expect(renderButton(page)).toBeEnabled({timeout: 30_000});

  // Positive control: enabled before the render.
  for (const control of controls) {
    await expect(control).toBeEnabled();
  }

  const downloadPromise = page.waitForEvent("download", {timeout: 200_000});

  await renderButton(page).click();
  await expect(page.locator(".app-shell")).toHaveAttribute("data-authoring-locked", "true", {
    timeout: 30_000,
  });

  for (const control of controls) {
    await expect(control).toBeDisabled();
  }

  await downloadPromise;
  await expect(page.locator(".app-shell")).toHaveAttribute("data-render-state", "success", {
    timeout: 60_000,
  });
  await expect(page.locator(".app-shell")).toHaveAttribute("data-authoring-locked", "false");

  for (const control of controls) {
    await expect(control).toBeEnabled();
  }
});
```

```markdown

Tora Video Engine is a small declarative video renderer and local-first browser authoring tool built with Remotion. The same validated `Story` drives the local CLI, Remotion Player preview, and browser-side H.264 MP4 rendering.

> Status: **v0.3.3 deployed** (v0.1 renderer, v0.2 Web Authoring MVP, v0.3 Local Custom Assets); **v0.4 Videos with sound is in progress** ([docs/audio/](docs/audio/README.md)): the audio fields (`music`, per-scene `sfx` and `voice`, `duration: auto`) are accepted, validated, saved and exported, and the preview plays the bundled sound effects (the Player starts muted; use its volume button), but nothing is rendered yet: MP4 render is blocked for Stories that use them. My audio (AUDIO-003): audio files can be imported into this browser's library (and the CLI reads `local-assets/audio/`), and local audio plays in the preview; until the bundled music tracks exist, unmuting a Story whose audio is only bundled music plays nothing, and imported audio is not mixed into a render yet. Production: `https://tora-video-engine.netlify.app`. The v0.1 reference render is accepted in [MVP-006](docs/mvp/MVP-006-reference-story-and-verification.md), the v0.2 acceptance record lives in [docs/web/](docs/web/README.md), and each release's production gate report is attached to its [GitHub release](https://github.com/juanse-dev/tora-video-engine/releases).

## MVP flow
```

*New:*

```markdown

Tora Video Engine is a small declarative video renderer and local-first browser authoring tool built with Remotion. The same validated `Story` drives the local CLI, Remotion Player preview, and browser-side H.264 MP4 rendering.

> Status: **v0.3.3 deployed** (v0.1 renderer, v0.2 Web Authoring MVP, v0.3 Local Custom Assets); **v0.4 Videos with sound is in progress** ([docs/audio/](docs/audio/README.md)): the audio fields (`music`, per-scene `sfx` and `voice`, `duration: auto`) are accepted, validated, saved and exported, and the preview plays the bundled sound effects (the Player starts muted; use its volume button), and both renders (`Render MP4` in the browser and `npm run video`) write the mix as H.264 + AAC-LC 48 kHz stereo 128 kb/s, refusing to start while any sound is missing or does not decode. My audio (AUDIO-003): audio files can be imported into this browser's library (and the CLI reads `local-assets/audio/`), and local audio plays in the preview; until the bundled music tracks exist, unmuting a Story whose audio is only bundled music plays nothing. Production: `https://tora-video-engine.netlify.app`. The v0.1 reference render is accepted in [MVP-006](docs/mvp/MVP-006-reference-story-and-verification.md), the v0.2 acceptance record lives in [docs/web/](docs/web/README.md), and each release's production gate report is attached to its [GitHub release](https://github.com/juanse-dev/tora-video-engine/releases).

## MVP flow
```

*New:*

```markdown

Audio goes in `audio/` (MP3, WAV, M4A with AAC, or Ogg Opus; one audio track, mono or
stereo, up to 50 MiB and 300 seconds). `npm run assets` lists each file's ref and
duration. `npm run video` copies the audio files a Story uses (and the bundled sounds) into its private staging directory, checks that each one decodes completely and renders them as AAC; a missing or undecodable file stops it before the render starts.

Remove the audio fields to render.") refusal in `renderStoryMediaOnWeb` (with its `bypassAudioBlockForTest` option), `scripts/renderStory.ts` and App.tsx's Render MP4 disabled reason (`audioRenderBlock`, `data-audio-block`), the constant in `src/audio/storyAudio.ts`, and the tests that assert it. *Change 3 of 3 - Old:*

```markdown
## Done when

Both render paths output the planned AAC track for Stories with audio, refuse to start with unverified or missing audio, and keep v0.3 output for silent Stories.
```

*New:*

```markdown
## Done when

Both render paths output the planned AAC track for Stories with audio, refuse to start with unverified or missing audio, and keep v0.3 output for silent Stories.

## Implementation notes

- The browser has no render without the bundled-audio store of AUDIO-004: readiness reads `useBundledAudioStates()`; the transaction takes `getBundledAudioStates()` at the click.
- The ffmpeg in this repository is a minimal build without the raw `f32le`/`s16le` muxers, so the CLI preflight decodes to a mono 48 kHz 16-bit WAV on stdout (instead of `-f f32le -`) and counts the bytes after the WAV header (`decodedSeconds = bytes / (2 x 48_000)`).
- The compositor-package lookup lives in `scripts/ffmpegBinary.mjs` (typed by `ffmpegBinary.d.mts`): it is plain JavaScript because Playwright's loader cannot import a `.ts` file from the `.mjs` test helpers.
- The CLI stages the bundled files under `__local-assets/bundled/<id>.<ext>`; `scripts/audioRender.ts` orchestrates staging, metadata, the plan check and the decode preflight. The shared plan of a frozen source map is `compileFrozenAudioPlan` (`src/audio/readiness.ts`).
- `disallowFallbackToHtml5Audio` is `true` exactly while Remotion renders (`useRemotionEnvironment().isRendering`: the CLI and the browser renderer, which never falls back anyway); the Player and Studio keep the default.
- There was no in-product post-render check before this spec (the "existing download check" is the golden test's ffprobe assertions): `src/web/renderedMp4Check.ts` is new and runs, before the download, only for Stories with audio (a silent Story is unchanged, AINV-6). The video checks (H.264, 1080x1920, 30 FPS, frame count = plan T) are part of it; for the audio it requires exactly one AAC-LC (`mp4a.40.2`) 48 kHz stereo track, a duration within `1 / fps + 60 ms` of the video and a bitrate above 0. The 96 to 160 kb/s range (128 kb/s +-10%) is asserted only by the tests of the native-AAC path and the CLI.
- Measured with system Chrome 155 on Windows: AAC-LC is natively encodable at 48 kHz stereo 128 kb/s (output 128 kb/s). With native AAC forced off, the web renderer registers its WASM AAC encoder (D-13): WASM AAC ignores the requested bitrate (measured 67-89 kb/s); accepted. That render succeeds and passes the check.
- The repository ffprobe prints the AAC profile as `1` (LC); the tests accept `LC` or `1`.
- The CLI decode preflight fails only on a non-zero exit, on a decode that produced nothing, on output past 301 s, or on the D-9 truncation rule; it does not use `-xerror`, so a file the browser import accepts (a benign trailing frame error) is accepted by the CLI too. It also passes `-nostdin`, a `file:`-prefixed path and `-t 301`, and the ffmpeg child is attached to the command's interrupt guard (Ctrl+C or SIGTERM kills it).
- `AudioDecodeCache` gives every decode a 30 s timeout and an abort signal: a decode that never settles fails (and the queue moves on) instead of blocking every later render.
- Readiness reads the duration of a local voice from its verified payload (the one the render uses), so a failed read of the page's own duration map can never leave the Story at "Audio is still loading."
- An audio Story over the audio budget (more than 64 distinct local audio files or 256 MiB) is `over-budget` exactly like an image Story over its budget: the Player and the audio timeline give way to the message (ASSET-004).
- `disallowFallbackToHtml5Audio` makes a Remotion-Chrome decode failure of a file that passed the ffmpeg preflight a bare `Remotion render failed`; verified with Remotion's bundled Chrome on Windows for an M4A voice, and exercised on Linux by CI's `test:cli-e2e` with the system Chrome.
- CI: `.github/workflows/ci.yml` `timeout-minutes` goes from 15 to 25. Measured on the development machine: the new system-Chrome spec file takes about 42 s in total (10 tests, one worker) and the new CLI renders add about 45 s (`test:cli-e2e` goes from about 14 s to about 60 s); a hosted runner is typically 2 to 3 times slower, so about 3 to 4 more minutes in the one `verify` job.
- A `Checking audio…` status shows in the render banner while the sources are inspected and decoded.
- Test placement: the new unit tests live in `tests/audio-render-readiness.test.mjs`, `audio-render-preparation.test.mjs`, `audio-decode-cache.test.mjs`, `rendered-mp4-check.test.mjs`, `cli-audio-render.test.mjs`, `audio-preflight.test.mjs`, `audio-envelope.test.mjs`, `app-audio-render.test.mjs`; the system-Chrome specs in `tests/browser/audio-rendering.spec.mjs`.
```

Those two tests are first run against the real AUDIO-004 code; a name mismatch is fixed in the test, not in AUDIO-004's UI.
